A current processing job blocks a new conflicting run; editing/retiring the draft fences old publication. Use the existing shared at-most-two-process registry and unchanged child CPU/memory/frame/startup limits; no unbounded queue, provider call or live inspection pass. Cancelled HTTP delivery retains actual worker ownership until its body exits. GET `/recipe-qualification-jobs/{id}` provides explicit status/readback; expiry/failure never triggers an automatic new execution.

Measure every permitted case through the actual OpenCV worker and approved calibration. Compare findings and observable-class counts with the declared case; preserve mismatches, quality/unknowns, source hashes and failed reports. Collect and recheck session/owner, draft/revision, runtime/data epoch, current calibration and all calibration/reference/purpose rows before and after slow work and at publication. The report and qualification are immutable, canonical-hashed and bound to recipe/artifact/environment/data/calibration versions. Required calibration source rows are recorded with revision/content digests and revalidated during approval and active reads. Exact case dependencies remain required along with recipe references. Missing/corrupt/expired/rebound reports or dependency rows deny qualification/approval, with no report-less fallback.

The job/report may finish while inspections are disabled but cannot enable inspections or approve a recipe. GET `/recipes/{id}/qualification-reports/{report_id}` is an owner/reviewer scoped read of the exact current permitted report. Passing development cases may make a synthetic/printable recipe eligible only in a non-beta environment. Public physical diagnostics always retain `physical_truth_verified:false` and cannot create a successful approval qualification; independent physical assurance remains separate required work, with no public certification switch. Owner approval remains an explicit second operation on the matching current draft; a report or job completion never activates configuration.

The workbench supports case entry, explicit submit, job/result inspection and existing separate qualification review/approval. Freeze the original body/key/version after an uncertain response. Editing cases clears report review, and reload/unknown jobs require explicit job readback rather than blind resubmission. All reported task results remain development diagnostics until actual human/physical/operational acceptance is supplied.

Qualification selection refinement before dependent implementation: starting a new run clears the current draft's approval-qualification pointer. Completion sets it to that run even when diagnostics fail. Only the current completed successful qualification may approve that draft; an owner cannot bypass a newer failed comparison by selecting an older passing run. Earlier reports remain inspectable for the same recipe revision, explicitly ineligible for approval. This does not retire or replace an already active older recipe revision while a new draft is being prepared; its original dependency/expiry/data fences still apply.

## Asset-erasure admission and version-cleanup contract

Contract completion before implementation, October 14, 2026: deletion preview/approval, storage ownership, job leases and honest partial receipts were unspecified. This is the reusable selected-asset erasure stage of the full inspection/subject/privacy lifecycle; it does not replace inspection/subject discovery, dependent-metadata redaction, orphan reconciliation, retention enforcement or backup/tombstone restore duties. Their full acceptance remains required.

POST `/privacy/deletion-previews` accepts 1â€“16 distinct server asset UUIDs under owner/recent-MFA authority. It resolves committed frame/reference assets, exact owned namespaces and metadata receipt identity. It returns an expiring immutable scope digest, source metadata versions/hashes, current data epoch and an owner/session/access-bound preview. No browser storage key, bucket, version list, URL or certification flag is accepted. GET `/privacy/deletion-previews/{id}` allows current owner review/readback, including a committed job ID; only the original current owner/session/access and unchanged scope/epoch/asset versions may confirm an unexpired preview.

POST `/privacy/deletions` accepts `{preview_id, confirmed_digest, erase_asset_versions_acknowledged:true, understands_remaining_metadata_and_backups:true}`, exact preview If-Match, durable idempotency, CSRF and Origin. Atomically mark every selected asset read-denied, attach the immutable job ID, advance data authority, write minimal opaque asset tombstones and persist queued job/audit/consumed preview/idempotency. No S3 deletion happens before this reviewed irreversible access-denial commit. Existing read/dependency/publication fences must reject the selected sources immediately. Erasure does not depend on unexpired data-use permission or enabled inspections. It cannot be cancelled after commitment or reset by a retry.

GET `/jobs/{id}` returns owner-scoped erasure progress. POST `/jobs/{id}/advance` acquires a nonrenewable service lease under a current owner and exact job If-Match/idempotency. It performs one bounded version page for one selected asset. The lease binds runtime incarnation, original storage configuration digest and immutable approved scope. A duplicate attempt returns status without repeating work. An expired lease may be replaced explicitly; stale completion cannot overwrite a newer lease. Already-authorized cleanup may finish under its service lease after the requestor signs out; no new scope or lease may be acquired without current owner authority.

List only the exact server-derived frame/reference key with ExpectedBucketOwner and configured MaxKeys. Reject malformed, duplicate or foreign-key version listings before deleting anything. Delete only explicit `{Key,VersionId}` pairs; never issue an unversioned DELETE that creates a marker. Include object versions and delete markers, including a literal legacy null version when actually listed. Validate every reported successful/error result against the requested set. Always relist the first page after removal instead of persisting a marker for a deleted version. Partial/unknown outcomes remain retryable with read denial intact. Empty, untruncated authoritative listing is point-in-time live-version removal evidence, not proof of all data erasure.

A job whose selected live keys are empty moves to `metadata_pending`, with `erasure_complete:false`, `dependent_metadata_erased:false`, `backups_erased:false` and `downloaded_copies_recalled:false`. Source metadata and related records are not falsely described as erased. Full erasure remains pending later verified redaction, orphan/recovery/retention and backup duties. A committed erasure cancel request returns conflict; no rollback of denied reads or delivered copies is offered.

Required configuration: `deletion_review_ms` (1â€“60000), `deletion_jobs_per_minute` (1â€“100 per stable owner), `deletion_lease_ms` (1000â€“60000) and `deletion_version_page_size` (1â€“1000). These are implementation bounds, not approved target defaults or privacy completion promises. Missing bounds deny configuration. The owner workbench must show exact asset IDs, scope digest, source versions, expiry, immediate denial and remaining duties before explicit acknowledgments/confirmation; unknown confirmations/steps reuse their original body/key/version.


### Owner deletion review, readback and uncertain requests

P01/P07 use the owner-only workbench to select 1â€“16 distinct committed asset UUIDs; this is selected-asset cleanup, not a subject-wide deletion discovery service. The preview shows every asset ID/kind/hash/source revision, the canonical scope SHA-256, preview ID, expiry and remaining duties. The browser validates the exact canonical scope digest before presenting confirmation. The review window is bounded from request start, clears on hidden/offline transitions, and requires both explicit acknowledgments. Changing selection or preview IDs clears approval. Confirmation binds preview ID/digest/version and a frozen idempotency key; an uncertain retry reuses all of them even when the local review window expires, allowing only server reconciliation, never a new approval. A definitive rejection requires another current review.

Readback of a committed preview recovers its job ID. Entering a job ID directly is permitted for an authenticated current owner, while returning a different job/scope is rejected. Explicit advance handles at most one bounded version page for one selected asset; an unconfirmed step retains its original job ID/version/key. No automatic polling, cleanup loop, unversioned deletion, cancellation-after-commit or automatic new intent is introduced. Navigation during an unknown outcome may lose local intent; retain the preview/job IDs and read server state before another action. Sign-out never rolls back committed denial.

The screen must label queued/processing/error/metadata_pending states without implying full erasure. Empty-version verification includes its check time; delete acknowledgments are not a distinct-object count. All full-erasure/metadata/backup/downloaded-copy booleans remain false. Unknown response schema, mismatched selected IDs/scope or invented completion blocks the action and preserves any uncertain request. Target mismatch and active lease block new steps; the owner may read progress. Missing original asset storage bindings require verified migration, not browser repair. The preview/job/step counters independently enforce the configured per-minute allowance.


Runtime-incarnation follow-through: the private processing job now stores `lease_runtime` (instance ID, epoch, artifact/controls digests and environment) when its lease is acquired. Every service checkpoint reads job and runtime together, requires the current local handle and exact stored binding, and checks expiry/token/storage as before. Completion also carries the current RuntimeControlHead into the conditional transaction, so replacement between the last checkpoint and progress commit cannot publish stale completion. Finishing a step clears its private lease binding. An old processing job without that binding cannot use an old lease; only an eligible expired-lease/new-version attempt may acquire a current one. Inspection-enable state is not erasure authorization, and requester logout does not itself cancel a previously committed cleanup scope.


## Inspection export jobs and ephemeral archives

Contract completion before implementation: W14 specified exports but not the manifest, tickets, cancellation, bounds or coexistence with erasure jobs. The existing generic job router must dispatch by the server-owned job kind, then apply that family's authorization. No client chooses a handler or storage namespace. Erasure keeps its irreversible/no-cancel rules.

POST `/inspections/{id}/exports` requires an authorized reviewer or that inspection's originating operator (explicit grants, never owner title alone), current inspection If-Match, CSRF/Origin/idempotency, `selected_asset_ids` (0â€“3 distinct owned frame UUIDs, within configured maximum) and `historical_export_acknowledged:true`. No pending observation is admitted. Freeze inspection revision, original software/recipe/calibration/policy identity, current data epoch, selected sources and requester user; a moving inspection or changed dependency stops preparation. Metadata is historical, never current pass/shipping authority. Source metadata older than the project 30-day ceiling is unavailable. Optional improvement/publicity use is not granted.

A durable `inspection_export` job shares `/jobs/{id}` and explicit `/advance` and `/cancel`. Its bounded preparation stages collect inspection-bound observation metadata, inspection-related access/capture business events and versioned human dispositions. Query one bounded page per step, disclose deterministic timestamp/event-ID order with unknown same-millisecond chronology, and omit auth/session/capability/internal-storage data. Every included row is revision/hash-bound; any changed/missing source invalidates publication. A configured scan/record/size ceiling produces a failed job, never a silently truncated successful manifest. Selected media must belong to the inspection and pass the existing binary scope/purpose/retention checks. Unselected or unavailable media are explicitly omitted; no permission to fetch them is inferred. The canonical manifest is at most 256 KiB and includes source versions/hashes, origin, omissions, outcome-as-recorded, separate human handling and historical/current-approval false labels.

Each preparation lease binds exact job/version, current runtime incarnation and monotonic/UTC deadline; before progress commit, recheck requester grant, current source/data and runtime transactionally. A current same-build/config instance may resume an expired lease with a new exact-version attempt after fresh authorization; an old worker cannot publish. Jobs remain usable only until their original nonrenewable deadline and required source permissions expire. Replay of an attempt returns current status without another scan. Owner/recent-MFA may read a restricted status or cancel, but cannot acquire media/export authority without operator/reviewer scope. Requestor role/session revocation stops new reads/output. Cancellation invalidates tickets and fences racing output, including completed jobs; it cannot recall downloaded bytes.

POST `/jobs/{id}/download-tickets` is a current authorized requestor operation with exact job version and idempotency; require the acknowledged historical scope again. Issue an opaque session-bound ticket valid for at most 60 seconds and no longer than job/source validity. Keep the capability only in browser memory, never URLs/storage/logs. GET `/jobs/{id}/download` requires the ticket in `X-Export-Ticket`, current session/roles and full current scope; Range/If-Range are unsupported. A ticket admits one bounded archive construction and is consumed before media reads. Ready means bytes were prepared, not received/understood. Lost/abandoned/failed delivery needs a new explicitly requested ticket after current state review; do not blindly replay a consumed one. Ticket issuance retries preserve the original key/body/version and cannot renew expiry.

ZIP archives are built in bounded memory using uncompressed fixed-name entries (manifest plus selected verified images); no new S3 archive copy, background output or transferable object URL is created. Archive bytes are capped at 8 MiB and each image retains the existing 2 MiB/5-second read bound. Share the existing scoped image-read authority and independently cap active export constructions at two. Recheck every selected source, caller, data epoch, ticket/job and runtime before returning any bytes; use a final conditional ready audit. Headers bind job ID, manifest/archive SHA-256, byte length and remaining validity. Digests show consistency, not authentic physical capture or delivery.

Mandatory host/programmatic bounds: `export_jobs_per_minute` (1â€“20, separate create/step/ticket/download counters), `export_job_lifetime_ms` (1000â€“3600000), `export_lease_ms` (1000â€“60000), `export_page_size` (1â€“25), `export_max_scan_rows` (1â€“5000), `export_max_records` (1â€“512), `export_max_assets` (1â€“3), `export_ticket_ms` (1â€“60000), `export_download_ms` (1â€“30000), `export_max_concurrent` (1â€“2). Missing bounds deny composition. These are implementation ceilings, not approved target defaults or operational evidence. Full request fulfillment, retention/orphan/restore handling and actual AWS/human proof remain required.


Export source consistency: only terminal observation records, immutable business events and immutable human-decision revisions are included. A nonterminal historical/orphan source fails preparation rather than guessing its final state. Source hashes/revisions are checked in bounded snapshot batches; their authorized redaction/removal must advance DATA, which is conditionally checked with the frozen inspection, current actor and runtime before publication. No app endpoint may mutate a terminal historical record without that invalidation. Selected media additionally carries exact current asset/observation/purpose rows into publication and delivery checks. Private job state is capped below the DynamoDB item limit (350 KiB); the completed canonical manifest lives in a separate bounded row. A ceiling fails the job and yields no successful partial archive.


Export workbench readback: GET `/inspections/{id}/export-context` exposes current authorized inspection revision, historical recorded state, maximum selection and a latest asset-ID suggestion without media authority. GET `/inspections/{id}/exports` returns at most twenty scanned same-deployment jobs per page filtered to this requestor/inspection. Its 60-second signed cursor binds user and inspection; empty matching pages may still have a continuation. This is discovery, not a snapshot or automatic choice of the newest job. Use it to recover after an unknown creation response and lost local intent. Reading context clears old local job/copy approval; reading a job clears creation approval. No shared acknowledgment may authorize a different displayed job.

A successful manifest requires complete terminal observation lineage, exactly matching completion events, selected frames present in that history, intact content-hashed human records and their complete correction chain. Missing/corrupt lineage blocks preparation; never manufacture a replacement event or human truth. Completed-job reads/ticket issuance require the intact matching canonical manifest, with no missing-manifest fallback. Archive paths derive only from server asset UUIDs. The browser bounds/validates bytes, SHA/header/job/manifest identity and historical flags, cancels its fetch controller on every exit, consumes a ticket locally before fetching, and clears capabilities/blob URLs on expiry, hidden/offline state, navigation or explicit forget. Already delivered copies remain beyond revocation.


Export capacity follow-through: reserve each next page's maximum row count in the durable lease before calling Query; use at most the remaining allowance. Unknown/expired attempts retain their reservation; retries of the same intent cannot consume a second page, and exhausted allowance fails without another query. Known scanned rows and reserved allowance are separate fields. Recheck current role between source batches and logical construction deadlines before continuing. Configuration binds the operational notice, capability-key fingerprint and existing read bounds as well as object/export settings; rotations do not recycle old tickets. Manual advances, private signed exact-scope authority references and the separate same-host driver are locally implemented. Current-source verification and actual-host acceptance remain distinct; evaluation/full retention/recovery are unfinished.


### Maintenance processing authority contract

Contract gap reported before implementation, October 14: requested_by/created_by identifies a person but is not authority for the required separate same-host worker. Each newly created inspection_export or confirmed asset_erasure job must privately store a server-created processing_authority record. A fresh authorized manual lease claim replaces it atomically with that claim. Idempotent readback never refreshes it. Existing jobs without a valid reference remain held for automatic dispatch; no identity is fabricated or backfilled from a user ID.

The versioned HMAC body binds job ID/kind, immutable scope digest, hashed cookie-row locator (not bearer cookie), user/session IDs, access version, session epoch, role, issued time and nonrenewable expiry. Export scope binds requestor, inspection revision/hash, selected asset revisions/hashes, data/config/build/environment and original creation/expiry. Erasure scope binds the original reviewed scope digest and contents, job creation and creator; a different currently authorized owner may explicitly continue the same irreversible scope. Expiry is no later than session expiry/export expiry and, for owner steps, the existing MFA freshness bound (its inclusive last millisecond remains governed by AccessService). Export roles are only originating operator or permitted reviewer as enforced by the existing executor; erasure requires owner and recent MFA.

Resolution verifies the exact schema, signature, scope and time; resolves the stored hashed locator through current AccessService state; then checks the same original session, user, access version, session epoch and required role. Disable/revocation/logout, expired session/MFA, key rotation, scope changes or unknown kinds deny automatic dispatch. Executor source/purpose/runtime/lease/budget checks still apply; this record grants no download, ticket, live pass, promotion or new deletion scope. It is never accepted from HTTP bodies or exposed in job metadata, archives, audit details or diagnostics. Private persistence is not authorization to run a worker or prove full privacy erasure; retention/redaction remains required.

Implementation must test valid owner/operator/reviewer dispatch; signed-field and scope tampering; copied cross-job authority; stale role/access/session/MFA and key; unchanged retry; explicit fresh rebind; absence from public output. Driver discovery, durable shared-runtime step admission and lifecycle/shutdown now have a local implementation; actual-target proof and complete evaluation/retention/recovery remain unfinished.


### Automatic same-host maintenance driver contract

Missing limits and shutdown ownership reported before dependent code, October 14. One separate non-daemon maintenance thread per configured application shares its existing repository, AccessService, RuntimeControl, export/erasure services and SDK/vision-resource ownership. Construction performs no I/O or thread start; the driver starts once after target validation and runtime startup. Inspection enablement is not a prerequisite for committed cleanup. No second application factory, live pass, ticket/download, promotion or new deletion scope is allowed. Historical decision evaluation now uses the same bounded driver under the contract below; full vision/qualification/promotion remains unfinished and unknown kinds are skipped.

Mandatory explicit programmatic/private host limits: maintenance_enabled boolean; maintenance_poll_ms 250â€“60000; maintenance_page_size 1â€“25; maintenance_steps_per_minute 1â€“120; maintenance_drain_ms 1000â€“60000. Each poll reads at most one bounded job page and attempts at most one eligible queued/expired-lease export or erasure step. The rotating resource cursor progresses past held candidates; discovery is not FIFO or a consistent snapshot. A deployment-global durable minute quota reserves an attempted logical step before authority resolution, guarded by exact job revision and current runtime; restart cannot refill it. This is a logical-step allowance, not a complete SDK/cloud-spend limit. Existing executor budgets/leases still apply.

Resolve the private signed original-session reference, then call the existing executor with exact observed job version and deterministic job/version intent key. Missing/revoked/expired authority holds the job; a legitimate explicit manual step may rebind unchanged intent. Terminal/completed/cancelled/metadata_pending jobs are skipped. Current runtime replacement stops old admission. Concurrent manual/automatic advances compete through existing conditional leases. Export cancellation prevents a stale worker completion; irreversible asset denial is not cancelled or reversed.

Stop admission through a thread event; trusted executor checkpoints run before new leases, provider phases and final progress. An already admitted bounded SDK/commit phase may finish after a stop request; later phases must not begin once they observe stop. A stopped step retains a recoverable original lease and does not invent successful progress. No network cancellation or remote effect recall is promised.

The configured composition is the sole shared-resource owner; ordinary create_app callers keep their default resource cleanup. Stop/join uses the explicit bounded drain. On success, close shared resources once. On timeout, mark shutdown failed and defer cleanup until the worker actually exits; never close clients under an active step or report drained success. Cleanup registration must handle the worker finishing concurrently. Driver status exposes only bounded state/counters/limits to a recent-MFA owner; no session locator, signature, job ID/media or raw exception is returned. There is no browser start/stop/configuration endpoint.

Verify actual background export and erasure progress, bounded discovery/fairness/quota/restart, authority/lease/runtime/cancel races, event-gated provider phases, once-only startup, normal drain, failed drain/deferred exactly-once cleanup and owner-only diagnostics. Local thread/database/intercepted-storage checks cannot close actual-host/provider/full lifecycle/F5/human acceptance.


GET /api/v1/admin/maintenance requires a current owner grant and recent MFA; default unconfigured applications return 503 after identity checks. Response: enabled, state (constructed/disabled/running/stopping/stopped/superseded/drain_failed/failed), local_instance_current, thread_alive, active_step, drain_failed, counts (polls/attempts/succeeded/held/errors), safe last_reason, poll_ms/page_size/steps_per_minute/drain_ms. Counts describe attempts and acknowledged steps, not unique objects erased or request fulfillment. No resource cursor, private reference, job identifier, capability or raw exception is exposed.


### Separate optional-improvement permission contract

Missing F5 dependency reported before implementation, October 14: operational purposes and labels explicitly carry optional_improvement false; no replay/dataset may infer reuse permission from those records, an operational acknowledgment, a reviewer declaration or continued app use. Add a separate, revocable owner-attested permission for 1â€“16 selected retained asset UUIDs, with an independently configured improvement notice, documented permission reference and explicit optional-use/limitations acknowledgments. This is recorded business authorization, not independent certification of consent, physical truth, reviewer independence, publicity or a legal conclusion. No use is enabled by default; refusal leaves normal authorized operational use available.

POST /privacy/improvement-previews accepts distinct asset IDs under recent-MFA owner authority. Resolve current permitted committed frame/reference metadata through existing scoped AssetReads; do not fetch images or accept storage locations. Bind exact asset/purpose revisions and record hashes, recorded origin, operational permission reference/deadline and storage configuration. Return an expiring scope digest with source summary, current data epoch and separate notice version/hash. The private preview binds original owner/session/access, current runtime/configuration and the earliest source/review expiry. Repeated unknown intent returns the same preview and never renews it. GET by preview ID supports readback/recovery, including a committed permission ID.

POST /privacy/improvement-permissions accepts preview_id, confirmed_scope_sha256, documented_permission_reference, notice_version/hash, optional_improvement_acknowledged true, limitations_acknowledged true and explicit expires_at_ms, with exact preview If-Match/Origin/CSRF/idempotency. Re-resolve unchanged source scope and current data epoch, original owner/session/grant and notice before commit. Expiry must be future and no later than source rights/retention and configured maximum lifetime. Atomically persist an immutable content-hashed grant and current permission head, consume the preview, advance DATA and write minimized audit/idempotency/quota. Never set optional flags on existing operational records or qualify a label/policy. No media copy, training, publication, background job, subject contact or external permission verification occurs.

GET list/current permission supports owner/reviewer metadata scope with bounded 20-row pages; owner-only actions remain recent-MFA protected. Public fields include selected asset metadata/origin/hash, separate notice/reference/expiry, owner attestation and false independence/truth/publicity flags, current head/version and usable/held reason. Never return object locations, cookie/session identifiers or private source rows. Current metadata remains readable for unavailable/withdrawn scope when the actor is authorized; usability is false and is not a media-access receipt.

POST /privacy/improvement-permissions/{id}/withdraw requires a current owner, exact head If-Match and unchanged intent; it atomically marks withdrawn, advances DATA and audits without requiring surviving source images or unexpired source permissions. Former requestor departure does not silently remove committed business duties; any current authorized owner may withdraw the unchanged permission. Withdrawal stops future dependent uses; already received copies/effects cannot be recalled. Reuse after withdrawal requires a new reviewed permission, never unwithdrawal or renewal of the old grant.

Internal authorization for future F5 consumers requires an explicit reviewer grant, exact current permission-head version, selected in-scope assets, intact immutable grant/hash, unchanged configured notice/storage, current operational permission/source metadata, no read denial/deletion/expiry/withdrawal and the original minimum deadline. Return current DATA/runtime/source/grant rows for epoch-bound downstream commits. All reviewed sources remain required; a changed member cannot be silently omitted to keep a partial permission usable. Expired/held permission never falls back to operation-only use. The separate immutable grant/head/preview/audit/idempotency records remain subject to full privacy retention/redaction work.

Mandatory private host/programmatic improvement configuration: notice_version (1â€“128), notice_text (1â€“16384), max_lifetime_ms (1â€“31536000000), writes_per_minute (1â€“100 independently for committed preview/confirm/withdraw kinds), review_ms (1â€“60000). Use existing bounded privacy JSON receive controls and finite source/transaction bounds; refuse an overlarge dependency set rather than truncate. Owner UI must preview named scope, distinguish optional notice from operation, confirm explicitly, preserve unknown writes unchanged, recover by ID, inspect permission and withdraw. Reviewer UI is read-only. Test notice/session/source/epoch/expiry races, role/MFA/Origin/CSRF/body/quota, idempotency, metadata privacy, immediate DATA invalidation, post-deletion withdrawal and unchanged operational flags. Full datasets/replay/holdout access/promotion and actual rights/provider/human proof remain unimplemented/unaccepted dependencies.

Optional-permission implementation refinement: retain original notice text/version/hash in each immutable grant and preview for historical inspection, even after configured terms change. Preview compatibility additionally binds configured review/write/lifetime bounds and runtime incarnation. Existing grants never gain lifetime from a looser policy; a tighter current lifetime ceiling holds an overlong original grant. Public deadlines are integer milliseconds. The owner screen fixes expiry from reviewed server time, displays source capping, and retries unknown writes with identical body/version/key/expiry. Recovery must match the exact preview or permission of the pending intent; reading an unrelated withdrawn record cannot clear an unresolved withdrawal. No current result is renewed by a rights grant; DATA changes require fresh verification.


### Durable dataset-manifest contract

Missing F5 contract reported before code, October 14: only a pure Example/manifest helper exists; durable admission/preparation, group ownership across manifests, holdout access records, restart and large-manifest transactions are unspecified. This stage supports the original 1â€“1000-example capacity and does not substitute a small transaction-sized dataset or complete replay/promotion.

POST /datasets/manifests requires reviewer, Origin/CSRF/durable idempotency and a strict request of distinct label UUID/revision selections, DEVELOPMENT/VALIDATION/FROZEN_TEST assignment and 1â€“2 exact improvement-permission head bindings per selection. Require explicit historical-diagnostic acknowledgment; selecting frozen data also acknowledges prior exposure/independence is unverified. Cap this route family's JSON at 262144 bytes and five seconds, leaving existing 8192-byte controls unchanged. Persist a bounded canonical request and SHA in a queued dataset_manifest maintenance job; no client groups, media location, code, source hash or truth/independence certification is accepted. Admission proves request syntax and current reviewer/runtime only; sources are pending until preparation completes.

Each leased step reserves a nonrefundable attempt and processes one selected label. Require its exact latest immutable revision/head/hash and reconstructed original source context. Derive kit/session/operator groups and asset IDs from server provenance. Every selected improvement binding must cover a needed source, and their union must cover all sources. Reauthorize each whole permission and source under current reviewer, notice, scope, expiry and DATA; operation-only labels never substitute. Original label truth/independence stays unverified. Conditional completion checks current DATA/runtime/actor, exact label and grant heads/immutable rows and persistent group assignments. Source mutations use the documented DATA protocol; no receipt may survive a changed epoch. Do not combine an unbounded source set in one transaction.

Persist one immutable content-hashed example per index, chained in order, and a bounded immutable final manifest header binding the request, count, chain, build/config/data, minimum expiry and current validation runtime. No truncation or partial successful manifest. Validate every served entry's index/hash/chain adjacency; missing/corrupt output holds delivery. Existing entry content may be reused only after the same source is revalidated. Runtime replacement during preparation restarts validation from index zero under the same original lifetime and nonrefundable total step budget; it never refreshes source intent, rights or budget. Completed receipts from another runtime/changed DATA/expired rights remain unusable; explicit fresh reviewed preparation is required. Cancellation fences in-flight work. Retirement preserves immutable history, advances DATA and invalidates dependent use.

Persistent group keys hash category plus server group identity. New assignments are conditional; an existing group cannot appear in a different split across manifests. Assignments remain conservative even after failed/cancelled preparation; retirement never proves data untouched. A recent-MFA owner may explicitly declassify an assigned VALIDATION/FROZEN_TEST group to DEVELOPMENT using exact version, reason and acknowledgment. Journal the transition, advance DATA and hold old receipts. No API upgrades a known development/exposed group into a stronger test split. Group identity is recorded provenance, not independently proven physical grouping.

Reviewer GET list/summary is bounded; owner-only GET /jobs status/cancellation remains restricted metadata. Reviewer request/example delivery rechecks current authority and records scoped access before output, including actor, kind, range and manifest/entry bindings when frozen data is involved. Access records are observations of these dataset deliveries, not proof that earlier label/media access never occurred; mark prior_exposure_unknown and independent_benchmark_qualified false. GET examples is paginated (1â€“20) and never serves source pixels. Source media/label endpoints retain their existing audit/role controls; complete cross-surface holdout assurance and actual independent protocol remain required before benchmark claims. Owner metadata access never implies reviewer/media permission.

Explicit endpoints: GET/POST /datasets/manifests; GET /datasets/manifests/{id}; GET /datasets/manifests/{id}/request and /examples; POST /datasets/manifests/{id}/retire; GET /datasets/groups/{sha}; POST /datasets/groups/{sha}/declassify. Generic /jobs read/advance/cancel and the shared maintenance driver dispatch dataset_manifest through signed original-reviewer session authority. No live policy/recipe activation or training occurs. The reviewer workbench must submit strict selections, read durable progress/request/examples and recover unknown writes unchanged; owner declassification/retirement are explicit consequences, not silent repair.

Mandatory programmatic/private-host dataset bounds: max_examples 1â€“1000, jobs_per_minute 1â€“20, step_attempts_per_minute 1â€“120, lifetime_ms 1000â€“3600000, lease_ms 1000â€“60000, max_steps 1â€“10000 (at least requested example count), reads_per_minute 1â€“120. Fixed JSON/storage/dependency/page caps apply independently; refuse excess rather than weaken checks. Jobs use current runtime leases, original expiry/configuration/permission/data and the shared global driver quota/stop/drain rules. Full replay/evaluation/promotion, physical truth, actual rights, untouched holdout, full privacy retention/restore and target/human acceptance remain separate unaccepted work.

Dataset-derived-data gap reported before dependent change: selected source-image rights do not automatically authorize reuse of associated human outcome metadata. Add a separate default-false derived_review_metadata_acknowledged field to an optional-improvement confirmation; persist derived_review_metadata_allowed in its immutable grant. Missing legacy fields mean false. The owner must hold documented authorization for associated outcome counts/method/duration metadata as well as the selected sources, and the notice/reference must cover that use. Existing grants are never upgraded; withdrawal and current notice/source checks still govern. Dataset preparation requires this explicit scope from every covering permission. No free-form review note, reviewer identifier or staff ranking is included in dataset examples. This recorded attestation is not actual independent rights/consent proof.

Manifest receipt_usable indicates current intact header, runtime, DATA/configuration and minimum deadline; consuming every example still requires index/hash/chain checks and the relevant current source rights. Historical request metadata may be read for explicit recovery even when old source receipts are held; those reads are audited and do not authorize reuse. Other reviewers may read business-scoped datasets but cannot advance/cancel/retire another requestor's job unless independently granted the required owner authority. Action hints reflect this restriction. A prepared example contains label outcome/counts/method/duration only under explicit derived-review permission; free-form notes, raw session/operator identifiers, media keys and client certification are excluded.

Dataset access rows record admitted data-delivery attempts before output; a disconnected receiver may not receive the bytes. They do not establish human reading, comprehension, influence or untouched-data status. Retain adverse/missing-delivery outcomes and do not use these records for employee ranking.

Final dataset timing fence, identified before repair: slow authority, dependency or audit calls must not bypass the original lease, monotonic attempt budget or minimum source/session deadline. Preparation must compare the exact current lease/runtime, preserve conditional source/DATA/actor rows, and check time after the final potentially slow check immediately before committing. Example delivery must log admission, then recheck unchanged dependencies and current reviewer authority and check the sealed deadline after those calls before returning. If expiry or revocation occurs during audit, withhold the page and retain the admission event. Neither a read retry nor a new lease extends original permission or job deadlines. These checks bound the application commit/delivery decision; they cannot guarantee receipt or prevent an already authorized recipient retaining information.

## Installed policy candidates and replay boundary

Contract gap reported before implementation (October 14, 2026; PP10/W16/T17/T19/T43/T58): the installed algorithm schema, candidate revisions and replay inputs were unspecified. Retained inspection JPEGs are cropped derived evidence, not original full-frame calibration inputs. Decision replay uses intact historical measured findings/action tuples; it cannot claim a new CV measurement, a counterfactual physical outcome or a new recipe qualification. Vision/recipe reprocessing requires separately permitted, qualified inputs through its own measurement/qualification path. Both types remain part of full F5; one cannot substitute for the other.

The first installed deterministic candidate algorithm is `reason_priority_v1`. Its only behavior parameter is an exact permutation of SEPARATE_REGION, PLACE_FLAT and RECAPTURE. Installed reason-to-eligible-action mapping remains fixed: overlap permits SEPARATE_REGION; not_flat permits PLACE_FLAT; blur/glare/motion/missing/extra permit RECAPTURE; otherwise REVIEW. Choose the highest-ranked eligible action. REVIEW remains mandatory when nothing is eligible, when the verifier/budgets require it or when a proposal is invalid. Parameters never change measurements, truth, allowed actions/instructions, current-pass authority, correction budgets or runtime controls. The default permutation reproduces the existing deterministic policy. This bounded proposed ordering is not a learned result or measured benefit; later development-only statistics and frozen evaluation must justify any promotion.

Candidate metadata has a server-created UUID, strict bounded display name, installed algorithm/priority, immutable numbered revision, content and behavior SHA-256, prior revision SHA-256, creator, creation time, artifact/controls/configuration/environment binding and a separate mutable latest head/state version. POST /policies creates a draft; POST /policies/{id}/revisions requires exact If-Match and appends without overwriting history. Reviewer role is required for writes. GET /policies and GET /policies/{id}?revision=N provide owner/reviewer metadata only; owner-only readers require recent MFA and do not inherit reviewer or media rights. Catalog pages are at most 20, with a bounded last-ID cursor that grants no authority. Operator-only/uninvited/disabled/foreign-deployment callers cannot read candidates. Candidate catalog access remains restricted. Exact-ID reads additionally support an ordinary invited-member approved view, defined in Approved policy by ID below; this never exposes a draft or private qualification source. Reads are accounted and reauthorize before output.

Retries bind exact original body/resource/version to the stable actor's idempotency key; lost confirmation can recover only by unchanged retry or exact recorded ID/revision readback. A changed body/version under the same key conflicts. A new head does not rewrite the earlier committed revision or make an old approval current. Concurrent revisions condition-check the same head; only one can append a given next revision. Source-free draft creation does not advance DATA or modify policy-active, recipes, inspections, runtime enablement or dataset permissions. No draft is evaluated, approved, active or eligible for promotion merely because it parses or is saved.

Private installation requires explicit PolicyCatalogConfig: writes_per_minute 1â€“100, reads_per_minute 1â€“120 and max_revisions 1â€“1000. Allowances are durable per stable reviewer/reader identity; missing bounds deny setup. Candidate writes, catalog/immutable rows, audit/idempotency and quota are atomic with current actor/runtime checks. Revision-limit or write-allowance exhaustion rejects before new candidate writes. If a durable write commits but its current readback is unavailable or read-limited, the response may be unconfirmed: unchanged retry returns the original revision after current authorization, never a new candidate. Immutable corruption, missing lineage, stale head/configuration, revoked actor or superseded runtime hold the operation. Old-build/config metadata may be read on the current service as historical, but cannot be revised; an explicit new draft is required.

Promotion/rollback and live policy reads must eventually bind the exact immutable revision and current complete evaluation, dataset/source rights, minimum deadlines, current DATA/build/owner authority and control invalidation. Those writers and the full evaluation worker/UI are not completed by the candidate catalog. No boolean request, client-reported success, unqualified frozen dataset or metadata read may bypass them.

Policy draft safe exit: an explicit acknowledgment may discard local retry details without another write; it neither determines nor undoes the server result. Keep the unresolved outcome visible, inspect the catalog with the accountable owner and require an explicit separate new intent. Reading another policy never resolves an outstanding write. Changing the visible target ID/revision clears acknowledgment and blocks writing until matching readback or an explicit new-draft reset.


## Historical decision replay and evaluation jobs

Missing contract reported before dependent code (PP10/W15/W16/W20; October 14): evaluation request/result schema, observational scoring, immutable job scope, restart/cancellation, exposure accounting and owner summaries were unspecified. Installed candidate metadata must say evaluation qualification NOT_VERIFIED, rather than assert NOT_RUN after separate diagnostic runs exist. No saved draft or diagnostic result qualifies promotion. Label duration is declared review effort, not measured operator-action time.

`decision_replay_v1` compares the installed default ordering and one exact current installed candidate on the same historical before-observation findings/quality/reasons. It performs no camera capture, CV reprocessing, operator action, model/provider call or verifier/pass update. Already-satisfied observable cases are counted as not_actionable. A proposed corrective action matching the actually recorded acknowledged action and an available after tuple can expose its declared resolved/unresolved label; a different action is counterfactual_unknown. Missing after/action, REVIEW-only escalation and incorrect_finding labels cannot supply invented corrective outcomes. Keep every case and denominator, split, origin, reason, recorded action, declared review duration and unknown classification. Raw counts may describe the recorded subset only; no counterfactual accuracy, operator throughput, learned improvement, independent truth or untouched benchmark follows. Full vision/recipe reprocessing, real comparison and qualification remain required separately.

POST /evaluations requires reviewer authority and a strict bounded request: runner=decision_replay_v1; policy_id/policy_revision/policy_state_version/policy_revision_sha256; manifest_id/manifest_sha256; historical_diagnostic_acknowledged=true; and frozen_holdout_acknowledged=true if the manifest contains frozen cases. The server derives count, groups and sources from the current complete manifest, revalidates latest labels plus exact separate source/derived-review permissions and binds current candidate head/immutable hash, installed algorithm, DATA/runtime/build/configuration, minimum source/job/session deadline and original budgets. Caller-supplied groups, metrics, certification, arbitrary code/URLs, model, budget reset or approval are forbidden. The candidate must still be the matching latest revision under the current installed binding; new proposals require new reviewed evaluations.

Preparation uses kind policy_evaluation in the existing maintenance-job namespace, private signed original-reviewer session scope and the same shared maintenance thread/quotas. Creation is queued; one nonrefundable lease attempt validates and replays one example. Persist immutable ordered result entries with content/previous/chain hashes, then atomically seal a final report only after all selected examples are accounted. No partial report is successful. Check exact actor, source/candidate/manifest/runtime/DATA conditions and original monotonic/lease/source/session deadlines after slow calls immediately before commit. Unchanged retry returns the committed job/current progress; changed intent/version conflicts and never adds another uncharged step. Missing/corrupt source, conflicting label/policy, expired/withdrawn permission, cancellation, exhausted steps or changed runtime holds/fails preparation without active effects. No new worker or model budget is introduced.

Explicit EvaluationConfig: jobs_per_minute 1â€“20; step_attempts_per_minute 1â€“120; reads_per_minute 1â€“120; lifetime_ms 1000â€“3600000; lease_ms 1000â€“60000; max_steps 1â€“10000 and at least the requested manifest count. The same instance/config must share access/runtime/repository with candidates/datasets. Top-level private host evaluation and ApplicationConfig.evaluation require these fields; missing bounds deny setup. Stable-identity quotas do not refill on login or runtime replacement. Original job/session/manifest lifetimes never renew. A runtime replacement invalidates completed input manifests, so existing evaluation progress remains held; explicitly prepare a fresh reviewed manifest and submit a new evaluation. Retain old progress/attempts/history and permit authorized cancellation; do not silently retarget/restart the old scope.

GET /evaluations lists at most 20 metadata rows per page. GET /jobs/{id} exposes control metadata and permitted action hints; original reviewer can advance/cancel unfinished work, while a current-MFA owner can cancel but cannot inherit submit/advance/example rights. GET /evaluations/{id} supplies owner/reviewer status and, only while current source authority/header bindings remain valid, minimized aggregate diagnostics. Owner summaries are an explicit metadata-review operation, never a grant to download images or read examples. Validate originating processing authority as a dependency, without impersonating it to authorize the owner's operation. Held summaries omit derived metrics and state the hold. A current report header is not proof that all result rows remain intact or that the report is qualified.

GET /evaluations/{id}/examples requires reviewer authority and current complete report/source scope, serves at most 20 immutable entries, checks index/content/adjacent chain/final-root hashes, and records dataset exposure before output with a trusted consumer job ID. After audit, recheck current role/dependencies/deadline before returning; withheld output retains its admission event. Historical request readback is reviewer-only and permits explicit recovery, not reuse authorization. No free-form review note, raw session/operator identity, media key or pixel bytes are included. Report flags remain diagnostic_only=true, independent_benchmark_qualified=false, promotion_eligible=false and operator_effort_measured=false.

Cancel unfinished/failed evaluations through exact-version /jobs/{id}/cancel; it cannot undo prior exposure and never refunds attempts. Completed reports require explicit POST /evaluations/{id}/retire with current original reviewer or current-MFA owner authority, exact version, acknowledgment/reason and immutable retry. Retirement preserves history, advances DATA and invalidates dependent current use. Completed or held results cannot be promoted through a boolean request. Future promotion/rollback must revalidate complete qualified evidence and live policy/source/owner/runtime dependencies; those activation paths are not completed by historical replay.

The workbench separates reviewer submission from owner minimized review, shows queued/processing/held/completed states and original remaining budget, supports explicit original-body/key/version retries and recorded-ID discovery, and clears source/approval context on edits, expiry, hidden/offline or unrelated readback. Compare observed matches and counterfactual unknowns plainly; no metric is a shipment approval. Leaving loses local retry details without undoing server work. An acknowledged safe exit may discard local intent but must preserve unresolved outcome and require a separate explicit new request. Human understanding, actual data authority and all applicable T/W/A acceptance remain unproved until independently evidenced.

Deadline follow-through reported before repair: reservation commits must check the original effective source/session deadline after their final execution checkpoint, before consuming a step. A terminal source-failure write must still own the same unexpired token/runtime/lease and monotonic interval after final authorization/checkpoint. Expiry leaves the already reserved step consumed and its processing record available for authorized later recovery/cancellation; it cannot create a late failure/completion report or refund the reservation. These requirements apply to every progress path, not only a successful report seal.

Dataset DF01 applies the same final admission and terminal-failure fences to DatasetManifests.advance: verify its original job/session lifetime after the last reservation checkpoint; re-read the exact token/runtime/lease and monotonic interval after failure-path authorization/checkpoint before writing. Preserve the already reserved attempt when a later lease expires. This does not change the dataset's explicitly bounded partial runtime revalidation or renew any lifetime/budget.

Camera playback startup contract reported before repair (W07/W10/W12/W26): getUserMedia permission remains explicitly cancelable and may wait for the person's permission decision; it is not a preview or observation. Once media is returned, video.play must complete within 750 ms and obey the current abort signal. A pending, rejected or late playback cannot set camera-ready, start upload, restore approval or retain streams after stop. The separate fresh-frame callback still has its existing 750 ms bound and frame/ROI/visibility/version checks. Timeout reports a retry/help instruction and releases the stream; Stop remains available during startup and must release the busy state without an old asynchronous error overwriting its message. Display an explicit startup state. Keep the same inspection and all correction budgets. This conservative local startup bound requires actual supported-camera evaluation; it is not evidence of physical-camera latency or a causal fix for earlier renderer intermittency.

## Selected original work-frame retention for full vision replay

Gap reported before implementation (PP09/PP10; W03/W08/W09/W14/W15/W16/W20/W26): published evidence currently retains only a rectified JPEG crop. Full vision reprocessing needs the exact transmitted image with markers, original geometry, recipe/calibration/software/source binding and separate permitted reuse. Crops, client flags or hash-only source references cannot recreate those bytes. This prerequisite does not complete the vision runner, qualification, activation or real-world benefit.

Original retention defaults off. PurposeRequest adds strict original_capture_retention_acknowledged=false and original_capture_notice_sha256=null. True requires the exact server-supplied original-capture-v1 notice hash, current general operational notice/source permission, current-MFA owner and bounded purpose expiry. False must not carry a hash; omit the new default fields from idempotency fingerprints so old uncertain create requests remain the same intent. Record the immutable original notice/hash/acknowledgment in the new purpose and expose it in owner/reviewer metadata. No old purpose acquires this permission. Changing configuration to use a newly approved purpose is an explicit administrator handoff; no checkbox enables inspections. Improvement/publicity remain independently off.

The originating operator explicitly selects each next original image. Capture PermitBody adds strict retain_original_frame=false. True requires the current configured attested original-retention purpose. Reserve at most three original attempts per inspection atomically with permit creation; expired/failed/abandoned permits do not refund slots, and replay/stop/restart cannot reset them. Include true in the permit intent fingerprint; false preserves existing request fingerprints. The permit, nonce and resulting observation bind this selection. The multipart image envelope stays unchanged; callers cannot retrofit selection at upload. UI choices clear after the selected attempt or interruption and never silently repeat across automatic observations. Normal checking works without optional originals. Context shows permission and remaining attempt count before selection.

When selected, store the exact accepted transmitted JPEG/PNG (at most the existing 2 MiB image limit) and its rectified derivative in the existing private versioned frame namespace. Use a deterministic separate original asset ID derived from observation ID, a durable distinct object intent, exact checksum/version/context binding and the existing bounded store adapter. Both selected assets and linked intents must commit with the completed observation before publication; an uncertain/missing original never silently falls back to crop-only success. Keep source MIME/bytes/hash, calibration hash, original observation/context and input provenance, origin declaration, purpose and expiry. Original expiry is bounded by purpose expiry and seven days from admission. Original flags do not certify authentic camera input or physical truth.

Retained original metadata/body uses existing originating-operator/reviewer access and authenticated proxied delivery; owner metadata/MFA does not inherit raw-image access. Legacy observations have no original and remain ineligible as full-frame replay inputs. Discovery returns at most the three successfully linked original IDs from the inspection, with current metadata authorization. Reads validate completed observation, exact original ID/source/context/calibration binding, purpose, DATA, runtime, deadlines and final delivery fences. Current publication checks include any selected supporting original. Labels and dataset sources preserve original provenance when present, with independent optional improvement plus derived-review authorization for every source; labels on legacy crop-only tuples are not upgraded.

The discovery route is `GET /api/v1/inspections/{id}/evidence/originals`. Its metadata-only response has inspection_id, originals (zero to three existing asset-metadata objects), common remaining_validity_ms, historical=true and machine_pass=false. Each asset identifies its derivation; originals use original_transmitted_frame. It reads only the inspection's bounded index and exact linked records, charges the existing read quota, holds the existing read-capacity slot, audits admission/read, and rechecks the full combined snapshot after audit. Duplicate, oversized, unreserved, missing or mismatched entries fail closed. An empty legacy list does not imply replay eligibility. Existing `/assets/{id}` and `/assets/{id}/download` enforce separate metadata/body authority and fresh delivery fences. The local owner/operator selector and evidence-discovery screens implement this contract; backend or synthetic browser availability does not establish complete physical workflows, full privacy fulfillment or beta readiness.

Historical export explicitly selects originals under the unchanged three-media export cap and validates matching completed-observation/event lineage; omitted originals are identified honestly. Selected-asset erasure recognizes originals as frame objects and immediately denies reads/advances DATA, using the same exact owned-key/all-version cleanup. Full dependent-metadata/orphan/backup/restore duties remain open, including failed original intents and consumed slots. No continuous video, broader camera area, automatic reuse, new S3 namespace/resource, deployment or spending authority is introduced.


## Full original-frame vision replay contract

Gap reported before implementation (PP10/PP11; W15/W16; T17â€“T19/T41â€“T43/T58): decision_replay_v1 does not measure pixels, its request cannot bind a candidate recipe, and an original's calibration hash alone cannot reconstruct the original geometry after configuration changes. Implement actual baseline/candidate OpenCV measurement; historical action-choice replay remains a different runner. This contract does not substitute count diagnostics for independent physical qualification, golden/safety acceptance or promotion/rollback.

New original records retain the exact validated CalibrationProfile JSON used by the server, plus its existing calibration hash. This is immutable capture interpretation metadata under the original's same bounded purpose/expiry, not client-supplied replacement geometry. Strict parsing, exact profile content hash, origin and recipe/profile identity must agree before replay. Legacy originals without geometry remain readable historical images but are not full replay inputs; never synthesize geometry from a hash or silently use the current active calibration. The original operation still requires its normal qualification at capture. Reprocessing historical sources does not assert that their old calibration is currently qualified for new live captures.

vision_replay_v1 uses the same evaluation identity, durable lifecycle, original reviewer authority, nonrefundable one-example step reservation, original job/session/source/lease/runtime/monotonic bounds, cancellation/retirement, source holds, chain/header integrity and bounded shared maintenance driver as historical evaluation. Request extends the existing exact policy/manifest bindings with candidate_recipe_id, candidate_recipe_revision, candidate_recipe_state_version, candidate_recipe_sha256 and vision_measurement_acknowledged=true. All identities/revisions/digests are strict; no paths, URLs, object keys, pixels, profile overrides or caller-supplied measurements are accepted. It remains unavailable through HTTP until its executor/factory/routes/workbench are integrated and verified; a component engine alone is not a delivered workflow.

For each sealed dataset example, revalidate its exact chain/content, latest immutable label revision, all original/crop source rows and separate image plus derived-review permissions. Preserve every authorization snapshot through the final measurement/publication fence. Owner metadata access is insufficient; the original reviewer must retain current reviewer/media authority. Frozen source use needs the existing separate acknowledgment and exposure audit; neither reading nor a frozen split establishes untouched independence. Record admitted case access before image delivery; an admitted event does not prove receipt or a completed evaluation.

Resolve the source's exact immutable recipe as the baseline and the exact current non-retired candidate recipe revision. Both must have the same kit SKU, declared origin and capture-profile identity; compare against the source geometry, never a new active geometry. Ground-truth counts must explicitly cover every class in either recipe; absence is insufficient evidence, not zero. Tolerance/dimension findings remain measured findings without independent dimension-truth accuracy. Candidate spec changes cannot redefine independent truth or authorize a different physical use case.

Read at most two selected originals (before/after) through the existing authenticated checked bytes path, at most 2 MiB each. Execute the full installed OpenCV 5 decode/rectify/measure/tracking path once per original per side through one shared diagnostic-worker lease: at most four measurements, baseline then candidate, equal per-call resource bounds, independent side tracker context, no automatic restart or new pool. Busy/dead/expired workers fail closed under the original reserved-step budget. No live inspection, verifier state, action acknowledgment, image publication or external provider call is written by replay. Discard generated crop bytes; public results contain bounded numeric/class findings, source/recipe/profile/software hashes and actual origin, not raw/base64 images or private storage locations.

For each side retain before/after measurements, observed class counts and quality/unknown reasons. Count agreement is assessed only for observable measurements and explicit declared truth; unobservable/unknown frames remain unknown, never successful zero-count predictions. Historical matching-action declarations and mismatched-action counterfactual unknowns remain separate from fresh pixel measurements. A completed report reconciles all examples/frames and bounds; no partial/held job supplies a successful complete report. Preserve failed, stopped and unknown outcomes. Report computation duration separately from operator time; no speed or physical benefit claim follows from baseline-first execution.

Use existing evaluation max examples/steps/lease/lifetime/quota bounds. The component measurement window defaults to 15000 ms and cannot exceed the enclosing lease; report content is capped at 32768 UTF-8 bytes. Source/permission/session deadlines can only shorten this window. Check the execution gate and current actor, DATA/runtime, candidate head, label/group/source/permission versions and all effective deadlines before/after every storage/worker boundary and immediately before return/commit. Source mutation or cancellation during work holds output; do not refund attempts or renew lifetime. Current source bindings must be merged from authorization results, not discarded after transient checks.

Owner report review remains metadata-only with recent MFA; detailed examples/media stay scoped to the original reviewer with current reviewer authority. Completion records actual new CV measurements and local comparison counts only. Independent physical truth, golden/regression/safety qualification, actual equal-effort physical comparisons and explicit owner promotion/rollback still require their separate evidence and gates. No replay completion can activate a recipe/policy or make beta GO.

Installation is explicit opt-in: ApplicationConfig.vision_replay and the private host document's optional top-level vision_replay object are null/absent by default. When installed, require max_case_ms (1â€“60000, no greater than evaluation.lease_ms) and max_report_bytes (1â€“32768). Reuse the existing application registry and maintenance dispatcher; no separate worker pool or changed lease authority. Authenticated owner/reviewer `GET /api/v1/evaluations/options` returns available runners, max_examples, vision_case_window_ms, promotion_available=false and physical_truth_verified=false. Default installation offers decision_replay_v1 only. Capability discovery is not authorization to create or read source details.

`GET /api/v1/recipes/{id}/draft` includes recipe_sha256 of its checked immutable revision, for exact candidate input binding. Vision case entries persist canonical content_json text and its content_sha256/chain rather than DynamoDB native floats; this preserves fractional measurement intervals exactly as JSON numbers. Readers reject ambiguous dual content/content_json fields, malformed/noncanonical/oversized text, altered hashes or chain adjacency. Historical decision entry representation remains unchanged. Public results contain parsed measurements, never private canonical storage records or original geometry JSON.

## PP11 controlled four-baseline evaluation contract â€” implementation contract

Gap reported before dependent implementation, October 15, 2026: `evaluate_synthetic.py` evaluates single-frame development findings. Neither it nor two-sided historical/pixel diagnostics implements T18's four complete controlled baselines. This section owns the additional offline fixture experiment; it grants no new public route, app role, production data access, live activation or physical qualification. The prior frozen verification has completed. The controls and local offline implementation are installed and verified by the 501-input/12-gate checkpoint in controlled-verification.json and BUILD-STATUS. This establishes the authored synthetic experiment only; complete PP11/T18 and physical/human qualification remain unaccepted. P06 executes the local experiment; P03 reviews exact results; P01 receives the recommendation; P08 challenges applicability separately.

**Subject and provenance.** Generate a separate, immutable controlled-synthetic bundle, with three recipes, the existing six conditions and eight seed setups per recipe: 144 episodes and 24 explicitly synthetic setup groups. Use a disjoint seed namespace (`1000 + recipe_index*100 + setup`) and retain v1 development files unchanged. New seeds and a content-frozen manifest do not establish untouched independent holdout, physical setups or independent reviewers. Record generator/response-model/recipe/profile/candidate/controls/software digests and actual OpenCV version. Validate exact bundle inventory, bounded relative paths and bytes against deterministic generation; do not admit arbitrary photographs, URLs, private app assets or a caller's physical-origin flag.

**Common episode model.** All four arms start with identical bytes, recipe, geometry, declared synthetic truth, action availability and capture opportunity ceiling. A frozen, inspectable action-response graph supplies the next synthetic frame; policies see measured findings/reasons only, never truth, condition names, response edges or future frames. Complete frames remain complete; missing/wrong-visible-variant/removal frames remain unresolved under these limited actions. Separating an overlap leads to its paired complete rendering; recapture/place-flat leave that overlap unchanged. Recapture removes the disclosed synthetic glare occluder; other actions leave it unchanged. These are authored response assumptions, not observed physical intervention effects. They intentionally allow ties, wasted actions and unresolved cases; do not tune them after inspecting candidate outcomes. The removal condition here is an incomplete snapshot, not proof of live approval withdrawal; T11â€“T14 retain their temporal tests.

**Four installed arms.** `single_frame_v1` measures once and records unused opportunities. `fixed_recapture_v1` uses RECAPTURE only when another unresolved observation, the same measured-reason eligibility rules and remaining budget permit; otherwise it escalates to REVIEW. It cannot gain an unsupported action that the other arms lack. `rule_priority_v1` uses the installed reason-to-action eligibility map and DEFAULT_PRIORITY. `candidate_priority_v1` uses the same eligibility map and a strict reason_priority_v1 candidate definition; freeze its exact JSON/digest before loading cases. A configured priority is not a learned policy or approved production revision. Every arm stops on satisfied visible constraints, explicit REVIEW or exhausted budget. An unknown/worker failure cannot become satisfaction. Use separate tracker contexts per arm and actual decode/rectify/measure calls; no cached finding substitution. Rotate arm order deterministically by episode index, retaining the order so component timing does not silently favor one arm.

**Installed admission bounds.** The controlled_baselines group in controls.yaml defines: max_episodes 144; arms 4; max_response_states_per_episode 13; max_actions_per_arm 2; max_observations_per_arm 3; max_measurements_per_episode 12; max_measurements_per_run 1728; max_case_ms 15000; max_run_ms 600000; max_case_report_bytes 131072; max_run_report_bytes 33554432; max_manifest_bytes 1048576; max_candidate_bytes 8192; max_json_depth 32 (duplicate/nonfinite JSON members are rejected). The two-action limit derives from the existing correction ceiling. Existing worker memory/CPU/wall/startup/input and registry capacity limits continue unchanged; frames remain at most 2 MiB. Acquire one existing diagnostic lease per episode, with independent arm contexts, and explicitly release it. A failed worker is not automatically restarted or retried within that episode. Charge each measurement before execution and check monotonic case/run deadlines before and after storage/worker boundaries. Missing controls, unsupported candidate/model, source mutation, malformed graph, exhausted bounds or incomplete required output hold the run and return nonzero; never omit cases or claim an incomplete report complete.

**Outputs and interpretation.** Write IN_PROGRESS before execution, one bounded case record per planned episode, and a final complete report only after every required arm/case reconciles. Preserve failures, unknowns, unused budget, exact source/transition hashes, measured class counts/findings/reasons, chosen actions, terminal state, calls consumed and actual component duration. Keep worker startup/input preparation separate from arm computation; none is operator time. Report complete/incomplete ground-truth denominators using generated counts plus visible-variant truth. Overlap/glare can contain a complete kit even when the initial frame is unobservable; the v1 expected_satisfaction flag is not a kit-completeness label. Partition final visible-finding outcomes into correct satisfaction, correct rejection, incorrect satisfaction, incorrect rejection, unresolved, technical failure and not-run, reconciled against all planned arm/episode pairs. A negative finding is decisive only with good quality, no unknown regions, no UNOBSERVABLE constraint and at least one VIOLATED constraint. Escalation or an unobservable complete kit is not an incorrect rejection. Any arm with not-run or technical-failure outcomes retains its raw counts and planned denominators but reports all rates as null with INCOMPLETE_ARM; a missing trial is not zero risk. Complete arms with zero denominators report null with ZERO_DENOMINATOR. These are measurement diagnostics in an authored model, not actual accepted/rejected customer kits; no unknown is coerced into success or error.

Include all four arms, paired differences and raw per-group results in the local report, including no candidate advantage. Any interval/resampling describes these synthetic generator groups only; tiny/degenerate samples require an explicit descriptive-only reason, not a physical risk bound. Report preparation/measurement counts and descriptive component times; correct kits per labor-hour, operator burden, physical dimension accuracy, service/network latency, willingness to pay and cost per completed kit remain unmeasured. Required flags remain diagnostic_only=true, physical_truth_verified=false, independent_benchmark_qualified=false, promotion_eligible=false, operator_effort_measured=false and machine_pass=false. Exit zero means complete internally consistent experiment execution, not a winning candidate, independent truth or release approval.

**Required verification.** Unit/contract tests cover every arm, identical starting subjects, eligibility/budget enforcement, truth-versus-observability denominators, separate trackers, transitions, unknown/negative outcomes, balanced order, exact candidate identity, path/inventory tampering, bounds/deadlines and partial-run rejection. Actual OpenCV integration must execute all 144 episodes/four arms, preserve raw output/denominators and reconcile calls and summaries. Inject storage/worker/cancellation failures without retrying/refunding observations; retain the failed run. T18/E07/E08/E13 remain unaccepted until their complete applicable evidence exists. Physical/generalization/learning advantage, golden/safety promotion and human A08 remain separate required work.


Controlled evaluation entry points: `python scripts/generate_controlled_fixtures.py` creates a new immutable `fixtures/synthetic/controlled-v1` bundle, and `--verify` validates it; neither overwrites an existing bundle. `python scripts/evaluate_controlled.py --candidate fixtures/synthetic/controlled-candidate.json` runs the complete installed experiment and writes an owned local run journal plus `evidence/local/controlled-baselines.json`. The CLI owns a temporary one-slot standard worker registry for the offline experiment, never attaches to or mutates a live application. The fixed candidate is a configured demonstration ordering, not a learned recommendation. Full verification adds separate controlled-fixture and controlled-baseline gates, with a 620-second process guard around the existing 600-second experiment bound; all original gate/test/worker limits remain unchanged. Hash the entire synthetic fixture tree and README packaging input in source manifests. Changes to these inputs make earlier whole-subject results historical; canonical status stays in BUILD-STATUS so README package metadata can remain stable.


## Policy qualification, promotion and rollback â€” pending implementation contract

Gap reported before dependent implementation, October 15, 2026: the endpoint inventory names owner promotion/rollback, but the installed candidate catalog is draft-only and publication still uses default priority. No current diagnostic job, controlled-synthetic report or requester flag qualifies a policy. This section makes the missing admission and transition requirements explicit; it does not install endpoints, a qualification writer or new controls. Existing no-activation notices remain correct until complete integration is verified. P03 prepares evidence, P08 independently evaluates where required, P01 decides, and P06 implements the transaction/verification path.

**Qualification authority and evidence.** Reuse the runtime's distinction between trusted assurance-writer records and ordinary HTTP requests. A qualification record must be emitted by an explicitly configured server-side qualification path after actual required evaluations; it cannot be constructed from an owner's success/independence/physical boolean, a score pasted into a form, a candidate draft or the diagnostic jobs' metadata. The required golden, regression and safety case inventory is installed/versioned separately from submitted results. A complete record retains every required case, failure/unknown, runner/artifact/controls/environment identity, recipe/calibration scope, exact candidate ID/revision/hash and definition, source manifests and grouping/exposure lineage, data epoch, permission/source versions, truth/review provenance, result digest, creation and earliest expiry. Missing required cases, unknown outcomes, failed safety/regression criteria, stale/retired/exposed qualification sources, unverified required truth or an unavailable writer deny qualification. Finite local tests do not certify physical correctness or independent review.
