Physical/beta qualification additionally needs actual applicable target/physical/human evidence, with its real provenance and accountable review; self-declaration alone cannot satisfy it. Synthetic-demo qualification, if installed, is explicitly restricted to that environment and cannot migrate into beta.

The qualification case plan and acceptance criteria are frozen before execution. Required uncertainty/precision and benefit thresholds cannot be invented after outcomes. Golden/safety replay and a physical/operator comparison serve different requirements; the controlled four-arm fixture experiment remains DEVELOPMENT and diagnostic_only, including when complete. A tied or worse candidate must remain visible rather than silently tuning fixtures or dropping cases. Any request to change verifier constraints, action eligibility, REVIEW behavior, safety limits or model execution is outside reason_priority_v1 and needs its own design/qualification.

**Exact approval subject.** Promotion uses the existing owner-only route and current recent-MFA/Origin/CSRF/version/idempotency protections. Bind the request to the immutable candidate revision/definition digest, candidate head version, exact complete qualification report and installed case-registry digests, active-policy head version, runtime artifact/controls/environment and control/data epochs. The client supplies identifiers and expected versions, never trusted outcome/authority fields. The server resolves all records and current dependencies and reauthorizes immediately before the conditional transaction. A changed head, input, latest required qualification, source, permission, deadline, account/session/MFA or runtime causes a hold/conflict without changing live policy. Owner-only metadata access grants no reviewer/source privileges. A lost response reuses the original idempotency identity and exact body; changed-body retry conflicts, and readback reports the actually committed transition.

**Atomic activation and live use.** Introduce one versioned active-policy head only through explicit configured installation; do not interpret a missing/corrupt head as a newly approved candidate. The installed default and each later revision have explicit identity, definition/hash, qualification/approval provenance, environment and predecessor. A successful owner transaction writes the immutable activation event and new head together with a monotone runtime control-version change and inspection disablement, guarded by every required current snapshot. Existing inspection/pass/proposal bindings therefore become stale immediately; no background scan is the invalidation authority. Recovery/readiness and explicit owner enablement remain required before fresh work. Source/time/authorization checks still apply at the final commit boundary; a successful preview or an earlier read is not approval.

Fresh inspections and publication bind/read the exact active-policy head and qualified definition under the current runtime/control/data authority. The chosen priority can only reorder the three already eligible corrective actions; it cannot add an action, grant pass, change REVIEW, refund budgets or bypass the verifier. Observation-time and final-publication checks fence a concurrent activation; delayed actions/ACKs/exports never restore old current status. Record the actual policy identity/mode in action and evidence provenance. No network/model side effect follows from promotion of an installed deterministic priority.

**Rollback.** Rollback is an explicit owner decision to the identified predecessor, not restoration of old inspection evidence or blanket approval of historical qualification. Revalidate the target's algorithm, current artifact/controls/environment, qualification criteria, source/permission/data versions and expiry under the same authority and final-commit rules. An expired, withdrawn, incompatible or failed predecessor is ineligible even when it was once active. Record a new transition and monotone control version, disable inspections and invalidate old work exactly as promotion does. Do not silently fall back to an arbitrary revision or re-enable the installation. Any alternative emergency default recovery must be an explicitly reviewed recovery operation with its own scope and receipt.

**Bounds and verification before implementation acceptance.** Add explicit versioned controls for qualification jobs, source/case/page/output bounds, original lifetime/lease/attempt budgets, transition/read quotas and transaction action capacity before installing the path; missing settings keep it disabled. Reuse existing bounded shared job/worker and repository patterns, not an unbounded scan or duplicate execution engine. Limits must cover all required dependencies; never omit checks to fit a transaction. Cancellation, restart, unknown completion and rejected publication retain original reservations/history.

Under W16/W18 and T17/T19/T20/T33/T34/T42/T43/T48/T53, test complete eligible synthetic-demo qualification separately from all beta/physical denials; draft/diagnostic forgery; omitted/failed/unknown safety cases; changed candidate/report/registry/recipe/calibration/build/config/environment; permission withdrawal/label changes/exposure; role/session/MFA loss; expiry at final commit; quota and budget bounds; concurrent promote/rollback; committed-write/lost-response readback; atomic event/head/control state; old live pass/capture/proposal/ACK invalidation; current-policy action selection with unchanged REVIEW/verifier/budgets; rollback to revoked/expired predecessors; restart and explicitly disabled/unconfigured operation. Add rendered owner/reviewer denial, exact-version confirmation, stale-preview, uncertain-write recovery and no-implicit-resume journeys. All these combined workflow results remain NOT_RUN. Record local, provider, operational, physical and human results separately; no local fixture can approve launch.


### Executable policy contract-suite prerequisite

Before installing qualification/activation, add policy_contract_suite_v1: an installed, source-versioned inventory enumerating all 128 subsets of the seven known corrective reason codes, explicit untrusted-reason cases, and safety/regression cases that execute the actual pure policy/verifier functions. Expected eligibility uses a separately declared literal registry, not the production selector's own reason map as its oracle. Cover every authority binding, quality/unknown/incomplete evidence, exact freshness/expiry, sequence/replay/order/scene/stability, terminal states, correction ceiling, ACK without pass and a valid stable control. Preserve per-case inputs, expected/actual results, failures/unknowns and full planned denominator. All six valid candidate orderings must be exercised in tests; seeded wrong-action and unsafe-verifier mutations must make the suite fail.

Installed admission settings are explicit: at most 256 cases, 15000 ms monotonic total duration, 8192 bytes per case and 1048576 bytes per complete report. Charge before each case, forbid reuse of one executor, fence original source/registry/candidate/configuration and check trusted cancellation/authority/deadline callbacks before/after boundaries and final output. Callback time is included; failure, source mutation, clock reversal, missing controls or exceeded limits holds completion without refunding attempts or dropping planned cases. Case evidence is an owned snapshot, not a mutable approval object.

This software/synthetic contract suite is a required prerequisite, not a qualification writer or activation route. Even a complete PASS retains qualification_eligible=false, promotion_eligible=false, physical_truth_verified=false and independent_benchmark_qualified=false. Actual image/recipe regression, current permitted source manifests, independent physical/target/human evidence where required and owner activation remain separate obligations. Do not accept this report, a controlled-baseline report or client flags as sufficient qualification. The existing single POLICY head will be reused by the eventual activation transaction; no second active-policy authority is introduced.


### Durable qualification preparation â€” implementation contract

Gap reported before implementation: the installed software suite has no durable lease, source-bound reviewer job or restart/cancel/readback path. Add a qualification-preparation service, reusing the repository/access, exact processing-authority grants and maintenance conventions. This is the preparation stage of the pending full qualification workflow, not a substitute qualification writer. No preparation result may set qualification_eligible, promotion_eligible, physical_truth_verified or independent_benchmark_qualified true.

A reviewer binds the exact candidate ID and a complete current original-reviewer vision_replay_v1 evaluation's ID/state-version/report hash, with explicit preparation-only acknowledgment. Resolve its currently permitted dataset/candidate/recipe/source bindings through the existing sealed evaluation guard; decision-only diagnostics are insufficient. Freeze the installed software case inventory/implementation and runtime artifact/configuration/control/data authority. Original lifetime ends at the earliest source-report, original reviewer session or configured job expiry, with no renewal after restart. Require the original attempt budget to cover all 170 software cases plus every source-evaluation example. Source count/hash/permission/expiry changes hold processing and report delivery.

Advance one bounded case under a conditional original-job lease, charging a durable attempt before execution. First execute all installed software cases with independent expected outcomes; then read and verify every source evaluation entry through its existing authenticated/current delivery path, retaining its provenance digest without claiming new pixel or truth measurement. This gives a complete, inspectable preparation chain over actual prior image diagnostics. A saved software PASS or user-provided passed flag cannot bypass case execution. Failed software cases remain counted; unknown/expired/canceled work cannot seal a report. Exhausted attempts fail the job without refunds. Changing intent conflicts; a committed-write/lost-response retry reads the original job rather than repeating an effect. Token/lease/runtime/source/actor/deadline changes at execution and final commit reject publication.

Store immutable bounded per-step entries and a chain/count header only after every planned step commits. Complete preparation means not_qualified/independent-evidence-required, not approved. Bind report identity/hash, original source report and candidate, registry/implementation/configuration, source counts and earliest expiry. Original reviewer alone may read result entries; owner aggregate metadata does not grant source/example access. Readback reauthorizes after storage and respects current original-session/source authority. Cancel before completion or explicitly retire a completed result with clear consequences; neither restores a pass or refunds attempts. Later trusted qualification must validate all required image/golden/safety/truth/operating evidence and owner approval separately.

Explicit installation bounds reuse the existing evaluation ceilings: jobs 1â€“20/min, step attempts 1â€“120/min, reads 1â€“120/min, original lifetime 1000â€“3600000 ms, lease 1000â€“60000 ms and total reserved steps 1â€“10000, at most 1000 source examples plus 170 installed software cases. Requests stay within 8192 bytes, entries within the software suite's 8192-byte case cap, report header within 32768 bytes and pages within 20 entries. Missing controls/services keep admission disabled. Tests must prove actual local persistence, exact-current source admission, original reviewer/owner separation, concurrency, uncertain commit recovery, restart without budget renewal, software failure retention, all-source entry coverage, mid-step cancellation/revocation/expiry, corrupt chain/header handling and no active-policy/data grant from preparation. Automatic dispatch/API/UI integration and full qualification/activation remain required follow-through.


Replay lifetime correction, reported before repair: execution completion/commit deadlines and retained diagnostic-report dependency expiry are different bounds. A completed image report must not inherit the transient case/worker window as its entire reuse lifetime. Track original source/permission/session/dataset expiry separately from the unchanged per-case wall/monotonic execution deadline. The evaluator must invoke the original case's final source/authority/time guard immediately before its conditional entry/report commit, even when dependency validity lasts longer. Legacy component measure returns its strict execution deadline; the durable evaluator consumes a server-owned measured-case result with both bounds. A report may be read later only while its original job and every recorded dependency remain current; no old report or job expiry is renewed or rewritten. All original worker, case and enclosing lease limits remain unchanged.


### Qualification-preparation HTTP, discovery and dispatch contract

Reported before integration: the tested core has no application discovery/recovery route, configured factory or worker dispatch. Install /api/v1/qualification-preparations only through the same access/runtime/evaluation authority. POST collection uses the strict PreparationRequest and existing Origin/CSRF/idempotency guards; authenticated GET collection offers own-reviewer scope by default or explicit owner scope with recent MFA. Own discovery uses a server-derived per-user catalog; owner discovery uses aggregate business metadata. Listing does not claim source/report currency or expose source entries; opening a detail performs current guards. Limit/cursor are bounded to 20/canonical UUID, with no caller-selected user/deployment prefix.

GET /options exposes installed case/registry and bounds with non-authorizing flags. GET /{id} returns guarded summary; GET /{id}/request restores the exact original reviewer's metadata intent without renewing authority; GET /{id}/entries pages complete currently permitted entries for that original reviewer only. POST /{id}/retire requires strict Consequence acknowledgment, identifier-style reason, If-Match and idempotency. Generic /api/v1/jobs/{id}/advance and /cancel dispatch by stored server job kind, never caller type, with empty bounded bodies and existing write guards. All JSON writes remain within 8192 bytes. Wrong role, unrelated user, missing session, Origin/CSRF mismatch, malformed/version/body mismatch and unavailable configuration deny without alternate in-memory state.

The configured application requires paired explicit preparation EvaluationConfig bounds and ContractSuiteConfig plus enabled vision replay, with sufficient original budgets for the complete software inventory and at least one source example. No defaults enable preparation or inspection. Host JSON accepts the paired optional sections with strict numeric fields; missing pairs, unconfigured image executor, insufficient capacity or expanded bounds deny startup/check-config before SDK/server I/O. The shared MaintenanceDriver optionally registers the same preparation instance, resolves its exact original processing authority and uses existing per-job leases, global step/backpressure, stop/supersession and drain/cleanup conventions. No second worker loop or automatic policy activation is introduced. Manual operation remains available when automatic maintenance is explicitly disabled.

Required integration checks cover real HTTP source-bound creation/readback, discovery separation, unavailable service, metadata-only owner, strict body/query/version/Origin/CSRF and uncertain-write paths; driver actual persisted progress/stop/original-grant expiry and factory instance identity/closed startup; host no-I/O configuration checks; and all existing affected authority/composition boundaries. Workbench and full qualification/activation remain separate pending work until implemented and tested.

### Qualification-preparation workbench contract

Gap reported before UI implementation: the preparation service contract does not define the complete rendered selection, review, retry and recovery flow. Extend the existing member workbench for explicit reviewers and owners; operators receive no preparation navigation. A reviewer reads the exact completed current vision_replay_v1 evaluation and installed preparation options before a separate preparation-only acknowledgment/submission. Derive candidate ID, source evaluation ID/state version and report hash from that guarded read, never a client qualification flag. Reject unsupported or incomplete reports, insufficient installed capacity and non-false qualification/promotion/truth flags. Show installed software/source counts, original lifetime and the remaining independent evidence requirement.

Freeze each submitted body, target, version and idempotency key through uncertain response handling. Disable changed/new intents until exact retry succeeds or the user explicitly acknowledges exiting the unresolved intent. Discovery/readback is not proof that an uncertain write succeeded. Original-request recovery restores identifiers only and requires fresh source review before any new preparation. Own and explicit owner catalog modes use their server scopes; catalog metadata never claims current source/report validity. Opening a detail refreshes current status; owners may read current aggregate counts but not original-reviewer request/entries or advance work unless separately acting as the original reviewer. Owner actions require fresh server MFA. Do not infer media access from ownership.

Show one-step manual continuation and refresh, explaining that automatic progress requires enabled same-host maintenance; do not claim a configured automatic mode from options that do not expose it. Keep original attempt counts, held/failed outcomes and remaining lifetime visible. Read entries only for a complete current report, in bounded exact-order pages with original target identity and qualification flags checked. Show software failures and source-integrity checks as preparation evidence, not physical remeasurement or qualification. Cancellation/retirement use refreshed exact state, explicit consequences and existing identifier reason/acknowledgment; no activation control is introduced.

Use existing account/session freshness, busy guards and review deadlines. Clear input/result authority and hide counts/entries on expiry, hidden/offline transitions, failed reads or target change; a delayed response cannot resurrect an invalidated review. Pending exact intents survive a hidden/offline transition for unchanged retry. Explicit sign-out/unmount uses existing cancellation/uncertain-exit behavior. Browser checks cover reviewer complete/held/failed reports, role separation, paged entries, selected-source admission, loss/retry, expiry/visibility, cancellation/retirement and malicious success flags. These are synthetic rendered mechanisms; A05-Q1â€“Q3 human acceptance and actual provider/host evidence remain separate.

### Policy activation core and trusted qualification-reader boundary

Gap reported before dependent code: the pending activation contract has no executable reader handoff, strict request model or transition limits. Add a local domain core over the existing single POLICY head and RuntimeControl. Do not install HTTP/factory/UI activation or enable live custom ordering until the actual qualification writer/reader and live-selection path are complete and verified. The core's absent reader denies writes; tests may inject private trusted qualification records solely to verify transition behavior. Such injection is not an implemented qualification writer or evidence of physical correctness.

The configured server reader must share the exact policy/access/runtime/repository services. It returns a typed owned snapshot: immutable policy-qualification record, all current dependency rows, earliest deadline and a final source/authority guard. The reader owns actual complete installed-case/golden/regression/source/truth validation; the transition core independently checks exact candidate revision/definition, installed algorithm and configured qualification-registry digest, complete zero-failure/unknown case counts, qualification/current-head identity, current runtime/control/DATA/build/config/environment, original expiry and all returned snapshot conditions. A current per-candidate-revision qualification head is mandatory, so an earlier report cannot bypass a newer failed/retired report. Plain booleans or a diagnostic/preparation report are not this handoff. The first core is restricted to synthetic_demo; beta/physical activation remains unavailable until the genuine evidence path exists.

Strict transition requests bind operation bootstrap/promote/rollback, candidate ID/revision/head version/revision digest, qualification ID/report digest, expected active-head version, expected runtime control version and explicit consequence acknowledgment. Bootstrap alone expects absent POLICY/version zero and the installed default priority; promotion/rollback require an intact existing versioned head. Rollback additionally names the exact predecessor activation event and must freshly qualify its candidate under current dependencies; old approval alone is insufficient. A candidate head change conflicts even for a historical rollback target. Reject unsupported fields, coerced acknowledgments/versions, missing controls, incompatible scope and excess dependency capacity.

Configured transition limits are explicit: 1â€“20 writes/minute, 1â€“120 reads/minute, 1000â€“60000 ms review/commit window, at most 80 distinct qualification dependencies, and the existing hard 100-action transaction ceiling including actor/runtime/head/quota/idempotency/audit/event conditions. Reject excess; never truncate conditions. One conditional transaction writes an immutable activation event, the versioned POLICY head, quota and monotone runtime control change with inspections disabled and prior enablement cleared. It checks candidate/current qualification/source snapshots and owner/session/recent MFA immediately before commit, including callback duration in the original monotonic window. DATA is not advanced by activation itself; the control change invalidates old work.

The qualification-record row itself counts toward the configured distinct qualification dependency limit. Strict integer schema versions cannot be booleans. Freeze activation configuration, policy binding and reader identity through the final guard; a material change holds the transition. The transient owner review/commit deadline is distinct from active-policy dependency expiry: retain the qualified source's earliest expiry on the new head, while enforcing the shorter current owner/session/wall/monotonic bounds for the commit. Neither lifetime can renew the other.

Uncertain response retries bind the original actor-scoped body/key and return the original historical transition receipt without a second control increment/event, even if later state changes. A changed body under the same key conflicts. Readback is explicitly historical metadata and cannot re-enable inspection or certify current source validity. Required tests cover actual local atomic persistence, missing reader, bootstrap/default constraints, owner/MFA, strict versions, changed/expired/failed/superseded qualification, changed candidate/runtime/DATA/source, excess dependency/window/quota bounds, lost response and concurrent requests, exact predecessor rollback and requalification, and no implicit enablement. Full qualification, active live-selection/publication fences and rendered owner workflows remain required follow-through.

### Genuine synthetic-demo qualification writer and cold-start contract

Gap reported before implementation: a preparation-only qualification path would depend on operational inspections before the first active policy exists. Install two explicit server plans, neither available for beta/physical qualification. installed_default accepts only the installed default reason_priority_v1 permutation and executes all 170 software cases plus 12 separately installed generated golden-image cases; it requires no operational dataset. candidate requires the exact current candidate and complete original-reviewer preparation, verifies every preparation entry, checks every original image-evaluation example as a regression, and executes the same 12 installed golden cases. Existing controlled-v1 remains DEVELOPMENT/diagnostic; it is never relabeled golden or independent.

policy_golden_v1 uses newly declared deterministic layouts and analytic synthetic counts, retaining generator/base-renderer/recipe/calibration/OpenCV identities and exact generated PNG hashes. Freeze its complete manifest and expected findings/counts/quality/reasons before any candidate measurement. Its twelve cases are complete; each class missing/extra (eight); blank ROI blur; unsupported visible variant; clipped ROI. Acceptance is exact declared synthetic findings/counts/quality/reasons and installed eligible action behavior. Expected correct uncertainty counts as a passed safety case; missing/failed/unknown measurements cannot become zero-risk or qualified. Shared rendering/calibration code and same-author expectations are disclosed: this is no independent physical benchmark or measured benefit. Registry or rendering changes require new subject/version review; never tune the frozen plan after outcomes to manufacture a pass.

Qualification requests contain only exact candidate ID/revision/head version/digest, plan mode, candidate-mode preparation ID/state/report and explicit synthetic-demo acknowledgment. No outcome/truth/independence/eligibility fields are accepted. Capture installed software/golden registry, implementation and configuration hashes, current runtime/control/DATA/artifact/environment, original reviewer/processing grant, earliest expiry and complete step/case counts. Use existing EvaluationConfig bounds and shared maintenance conventions with a separate policy_qualification kind; reserve each durable attempt before work, conditional leases and immutable chained entries, no refund/renewal, strict cancellation/supersession and exact-intent recovery. All 170+N+12 required logical cases remain in the denominator; candidate preparation pages are at most 20 entries, source regressions one example each, and golden measurements one installed case each. Full 1000-example scope must fit the requested original budget; reject excess rather than truncate.

An admitted new job immediately updates the per-candidate-revision current-qualification head to pending, preventing fallback to an older report after a new failure/unknown. Complete reports retain qualified/rejected outcomes and every failure/unknown, sealed software/source/golden hashes and earliest source expiry. Only an actual complete zero-failure/unknown server run may emit a qualified synthetic_demo_only record; original preparation/diagnostic records keep their existing non-authorizing flags. For installed_default the preparation-report field binds the actually executed software-phase summary, explicitly tagged executed_installed_default_contracts, not a fabricated operational preparation. A missing case, technical interruption, stale source or incomplete job cannot emit qualification.

The genuine reader revalidates the original processing/source authority, exact current job and qualification head, immutable report/chain/installed inventory, complete outcomes and current dependencies; it returns the typed QualifiedPolicySnapshot to the existing activation core without granting owners reviewer/media access. Original reviewer entries remain bounded; aggregate metadata is separate. Cancellation/retirement preserve attempts/history and invalidate dependent use; no autonomous promotion, physical flag, learned/effort advantage or automatic inspection enablement follows. Actual writer-to-reader-to-activation tests must use emitted reports, separately from test-injected transition fixtures. HTTP/factory/worker/workbench and live-selection installation remain required after this service verification.

The default software suite runs as one bounded phase through PolicyContractSuite, retaining its unchanged 15000 ms whole-suite/callback ceiling. Reserve all 170 logical cases durably before executing it; a failed/retried phase never refunds that reservation. Separate configured max_reserved_cases (at most 10000) from leased step attempts. Persist its complete bounded report in immutable pages of at most 20 cases and 196608 bytes per page, plus a sealed phase header; never place a potentially 1 MiB report into one DynamoDB item. Candidate preparation pages charge their included software cases; source and golden phases each charge their own cases. Technical interruption cannot publish a complete phase or qualification.

Case/page records are append-only under this service (conditional version-one writes). Qualification readers stream and verify the complete immutable chain and phase records, then revalidate them in their final guard. The transaction conditions retain every current mutable authority/dependency and the sealed job/report/current head; they do not pretend to condition-check more than 100 individual immutable evidence rows in one transaction. No mutable source check may be dropped to fit capacity. Source edits, withdrawals, cleanup and retirement must advance their existing authority heads/DATA as appropriate. Full current-source and trusted-storage/IAM acceptance remain separate from local chain checks.

Original reviewers may inspect bounded qualification entries and every software-case page, including rejected outcomes; owners receive current aggregate metadata, not entry/media privileges. Explicit cancellation/retirement has a separate bounded end quota capped by configured jobs/minute, so an exhausted processing-rate window cannot prevent authorized stopping. Retirement requires acknowledged=true and an identifier reason, preserves records/attempts and advances DATA; cancellation cannot retire completed work. Expired source reports may remain controllable metadata but never regain eligibility through these operations.

Integration conflict reported before live-policy work: an unconsumed qualification is bound to its execution control version. Successful activation intentionally advances control, so ordinary read_current then holds that historical job. Do not relax this check globally or treat a newly stale report as a current reusable authorization. The separate internal active-policy validator uses the exact immutable activation event to distinguish the consumed qualification from a new promotion decision, revalidates current source/rights/DATA/build/owner conditions and accounts for authorized control changes. Downstream inspection admission and publication must consume that current snapshot; until those integrations are installed, retain inspection disablement and do not advertise a working live-policy workflow.

Active consumption implementation contract: provide a separate internal active-policy snapshot, never a QualifiedPolicySnapshot or request-supplied stale-control flag. Bind the current POLICY row to its exact immutable activation event, original genuine qualification job/report, execution control and unchanged runtime incarnation/artifact/controls/DATA. Subsequent control increments may be accepted only under that same current POLICY/event; none renews original source/session/report expiry. Re-run native qualification/preparation/evaluation source, registry, outcome and original-reviewer guards. A scoped server-only consumption context may account for the exact activation's control increment and later draft-head advancement for the same immutable qualified policy revision. It must not relax source recipe, rights, label, report, retirement, supersession or new-promotion checks. A draft save alone has no live effect, but a change during a read/commit must invalidate that snapshot. Current approving-owner membership, owner role and exact access version remain required; the earlier owner session/MFA is evidence of the transition rather than a perpetual login requirement. Return complete bounded dependencies, original expiry and a final revalidation callback for downstream conditional publication. Missing or excess dependencies deny rather than truncate.

Shared grant defect reported before repair: policy_qualification was signed as a supported reviewer job but omitted from issuer/resolver requester and job-expiry checks. Add the kind to all three checks; keep the writer's independent checks. Test ordinary exact grants, rejected foreign requester issuance, correctly signed but semantically mismatched requester/expiry, deadline equality and unchanged progress. These server-contract probes do not imply an external ability to sign grants.

Compatibility for the grant repair is fail-closed: an earlier stored grant that exceeds its original job deadline or names the wrong requester is held by the corrected resolver. Do not re-sign it, extend expiry, refund cases or rewrite its history to make it eligible. Current permitted inputs require a newly admitted job under the ordinary original budget/rate/authority rules. Authorized metadata cancellation/retirement stays separate from source qualification; no historical report is silently upgraded.

Live policy integration gaps reported before implementation: StationService has no trusted reader dependency, inspections retain only a mutable-head version, and publication still calls the installed default priority. Supply a server-owned reader sharing exact access/repository/runtime authority and returning ActivePolicySnapshot. An absent reader holds inspection admission and active authority; no legacy metadata-only fallback or request-selectable test mode is permitted. A test may explicitly inject a private diagnostic reader, disclosed as injected assurance rather than genuine qualification. The configured application remains closed until genuine qualification/activation composition is installed.

Every new inspection retains policy_provenance: policy ID/revision/revision SHA, behavior SHA, activation ID/event SHA, qualification ID/report SHA, plus its existing policy_state_version. Admission binds the exact read policy and earliest qualified/readiness/recipe/calibration/session expiry. Active reads require that same provenance and include every qualification dependency in the existing conditional authority scope. Current priority must come from the bound definition, never a default after a failed read. Persist the same provenance on the permit/observation/action and completion audit; a historical action remains historical and cannot restore authority. Keep existing allowed templates, REVIEW fallback, two-request budget, verifier/stability/lease limits and manual disposition unchanged.

Recheck the consumed policy after the final actor authorization read at admission, permit/admission, worker preparation, context/status delivery, publication and action acknowledgment. Before final publication, bind the exact source snapshot used for selection, original wall/monotonic observation deadline and all current dependency rows; changed/missing/expired source or configuration prevents a result/action commit. Extra stored media after authority loss remains unlinked and subject to ordinary reconciliation. Never truncate dependencies to fit DynamoDB capacity. Closing/stopping work remains possible without renewing an invalid policy. Test default closure, genuine activation followed by separately injected local readiness, current priority/provenance, lost/changed rights, deadline/configuration races, status withdrawal and immutable historical readback.


Capture context compatibility: inspection_context is shared by capture, historical label review and retained-image reads. New observations hash the complete normalized eight-field policy provenance and must match their originating inspection; partial/mismatched provenance denies. An older inspection and observation both lacking that field retain their original digest for historical review only. This compatibility never supplies live station authority. Canonical conversion must normalize persisted Decimal revision numbers before JSON encoding. A permit whose provenance differs from its inspection is unusable and cannot restore a nonce on readback.

The live integration test's original historical recipe is correctly inadmissible after review-data changes advance DATA. Preserve that failure; new operation requires fresh current operating qualification. The corrected local test creates a separate synthetic profile/recipe and explicit private calibration/recipe/readiness witnesses at the current epoch, without rewriting or extending old records. These are disclosed injected prerequisites, not physical qualification, provider readiness or independent truth. Genuine policy software/golden/source execution is assessed separately.


## Configured qualification and active-policy delivery contract

Initial missing integration reported October 15, 2026 before changing application composition (historical gap, partly implemented below): the local qualification writer, atomic activation core, consumed reader and live bridge exist, but ApplicationConfig/host input, configured factory, qualification/activation HTTP and workbench, and qualification maintenance dispatch do not yet deliver their complete role journeys. Existing preparation HTTP/worker/workbench paths are separate and already have scoped local evidence. Direct service tests do not install these paths. The following contract closes the specification gap; implementation and full workflow acceptance remain pending.

1. **Explicit paired configuration.** Typed qualification budgets/software suite and activation limits must be supplied together and validated before provider-client construction or startup. Bind the activation registry to the exact installed writer registry, software implementation, golden plan and controls; reject mismatch, incomplete prerequisite composition or insufficient complete-case capacity. Absent configuration keeps new inspection admission held. No credential discovery, beta-to-demo conversion, installed default activation or inspection enablement may follow from validation. The private host schema must carry these values without accepting client assurance booleans or a browser-supplied trusted reader.
2. **One shared composition.** Build policies, datasets, evaluation and optional preparation using existing shared access/runtime/repository/worker instances, then the genuine qualification writer, its activation reader and ActivePolicies. Construct StationService with that exact trusted reader before capture/observation services. Preserve single shutdown ownership, worker bounds and cleanup on construction/startup failure. A metadata-only head, test-injected reader or absent/corrupt event cannot replace the genuine chain in configured production composition.
3. **Delivered reviewer and owner paths.** Expose existing bounded qualification create/catalog/detail/request/case-page/step/cancel/retire semantics through typed authenticated APIs; expose owner-only current active metadata and explicit bootstrap/promote/rollback with recent MFA, Origin/CSRF, expected versions and immutable idempotency. Owners obtain only permitted aggregate metadata unless independently granted reviewer access. Do not accept report bytes, qualification outcomes, physical/independence flags, current-policy state or trusted-source attestations from request payloads.
4. **Worker and interruption semantics.** Qualification processing uses the existing exact-scope job authority and original deadlines, leases, attempts and case budgets. Explicit same-host configuration controls automatic dispatch; manual stepping remains bounded. Source/permission/runtime/configuration/session changes hold processing and delivery. A lost response retains exact request/body/key; reading a job does not resolve an uncertain write or renew a report. Preserve cancellation/retirement and shutdown fences; no queue retry can invent truth or activate a policy.
5. **Usable stage handoff.** P03 sees every failed/unknown case, synthetic origin and remaining physical obligations. P01 reviews the exact immutable candidate/report/current head before a separately acknowledged transition, with uncertain-response readback and no implicit retry after changed preview. Activation disables inspections atomically; explicit readiness and owner enablement remain separate. P02 sees loss of current approval and can stop/manual-check; P04 receives permitted unresolved context. No missing installed path directs a user to a private console as a substitute.
6. **Verification and evidence.** Before acceptance, exercise absent/malformed paired configuration, no-I/O validation, shared identity and cleanup, real qualified default/candidate consumption, bootstrap/promote/rollback, owner/reviewer/operator boundaries, exact retry/readback, source/head/expiry changes at final reads/commits, disabled admission and separate enablement, worker restart/cancel/drain, rendered held/failed/unknown/current states and temporary-access closure. Preserve original assertions, budgets, deadlines and failures. Bind tests to exact source/config/artifact/registry and reconcile complete inventories. A05-Q1â€“Q4/A05-L1/A06-L1 and existing T/W scopes still require the appropriate delivered, provider, physical and human evidence; synthetic service reports cannot close those obligations or beta gates.


Live corrective-action coverage limitation, observed October 15: vision/pipeline.py currently emits unknown_or_overlap for its ambiguous region and has no measured not_flat output. The installed reason_priority_v1 eligibility maps explicit overlap/not_flat to SEPARATE_REGION/PLACE_FLAT. The live-policy integration scenario observes the actual bound non-default priority tuple but its missing-part case has only RECAPTURE eligible, so it does not demonstrate a changed outcome, useful selection among multiple corrective actions, physical correction or adaptive benefit. Do not map ambiguity to a certain overlap, invent a flatness finding or widen eligibility to manufacture a successful demo. Complete PP05/PP07/F3 by defining and qualifying observable reason/region evidence, delivered specific overlays and same-kit correction/recheck behavior under the existing permitted action contract; independent physical/operator evidence remains required.


Configured-delivery HTTP/configuration details (specified before implementation): host JSON optionally adds paired policy_qualification (jobs with existing evaluation bounds, max_reserved_cases, golden_case_ms) and policy_activation (writes_per_minute, reads_per_minute, review_window_ms, max_qualification_rows). Full preparation/suite/vision composition is required for installation. Host loading derives the expected qualification registry SHA from the installed software/golden plan, never client assertions; typed ApplicationConfig carries that exact digest and construction rechecks it against the actual writer. Computing the deterministic synthetic plan for configuration is not qualification execution or enablement. Missing/invalid pairs and capacities below the installed default plan fail before provider use.

GET/POST /policy-qualifications, GET /policy-qualifications/options, /{id}, /{id}/request, /{id}/entries and /{id}/software-cases, and POST /{id}/retire deliver bounded reviewer/owner discovery and existing writer operations. Catalog scope is explicitly own (original reviewer) or owner (recent MFA); at most 20 rows, exact cursor, no scan-to-fill. Catalog currency is unchecked until opening detail; request recovery returns the exact original selections without renewing sources or settling an uncertain write. Generic /jobs/{id}, /advance and /cancel and the shared maintenance driver dispatch the genuine policy_qualification kind through existing signed reviewer authority.

GET /policies/active supplies an approved-head summary to invited members, without source/media access or permission to activate. Explicit scope=owner adds current head/control/predecessor metadata only under recent owner MFA. Current qualification is a fresh trusted-reader result, not inferred from a historical head/event; unavailable sources yield held/not-current metadata or deny malformed state. GET /policy-activations/{id} remains an owner-MFA historical receipt read. POST /policies/{id}/bootstrap, /promote and /rollback require a strict ActivationRequest matching path/operation and If-Match equal to its positive candidate policy_state_version. Bootstrap alone names expected_active_version=0; other active and runtime versions remain explicit body fields checked atomically by the existing core. No generic positive If-Match rule is weakened. Header/body mismatch or changed intent cannot transition. Static /policies/active must register before UUID candidate routes. All new JSON surfaces retain the existing bounded body guard, role/deployment/CSRF/Origin/idempotency checks and original deadlines/budgets.

### Qualification progress and local worker commits — missing coordination contract

Reported before repair: the unchanged configured-factory test observes STATE_CONFLICT from a job GET while the shared maintenance worker makes ordinary progress. The metadata reader correctly fences its job/source snapshots, but the contract lacks local coordination for that expected progress. Do not relax those equality checks, treat a conflict as a successful report, silently retry or extend a lease/report/session deadline.

Within the one configured qualification-service instance, coordinate the complete job-metadata read against the short durable create/reserve/finalize/cancel/retire transactions. An instance-local reentrant gate suffices; it is not a distributed lock or authority. Expensive software/vision execution remains outside the gate so a processing job can be inspected while its worker runs. Gate acquisition is bounded by the existing configured job lease duration and, when an actor is available, its remaining session lifetime. Expiry or saturation fails closed. All caller/source/configuration and original case/lease checks run after waiting and before the existing transaction/delivery boundary; waiting grants no new validity or attempts. No new queue, worker, paid resource or automatic retry is introduced.

Other service instances and external source/permission/runtime changes remain subject to unchanged transactional conditions and final snapshots, and may still return a conflict. The original stable-200 factory oracle remains unchanged. Verify actual local read/commit exclusion, exception/reentrant release, bounded waiting, worker execution outside the gate and preserved cross-instance/source race denial; then rerun genuine factory/HTTP/worker execution rather than substituting an injected report.

Implementation follow-through: local coordination and the required negative/deadline checks now have passing native evidence. The literal original configured factory oracle also passes from an offline installed package with a genuine local worker and provider sends blocked. These results apply only to the recorded synthetic/local configured ASGI scope. See [build status](BUILD-STATUS.md) for exact cohorts, package identity, retained failures and remaining host/provider/physical/human obligations.


### Approved policy by ID — delivery clarification before implementation

Reported gap: the original invited-member approved-policy read is still absent. GET /policies/{id} currently selects candidate-only authority; GET /policies/active does not fulfill exact-ID lookup. The safe read contract is the recorded current active head, not a public candidate catalog or private historical qualification search.

GET /policies/{id}?view=approved returns the same minimized summary shape and current/held semantics as GET /policies/active, bound to that exact active policy ID. An optional revision must equal the recorded active revision; absent/different IDs or revisions return unavailable without exposing draft existence. Approval records an owner transition; current qualification is verified separately and no read enables inspection. Held current-head metadata may be shown with current=false and remaining_ms=0. Retired/superseded policies are not discoverable through this read. No candidate definition, source selectors, media, owner control versions or activation/qualification identifiers are returned.

Without an explicit view, owner/reviewer accounts retain their existing candidate view; an ordinary invited member receives the approved view. Explicit view=candidate retains the original role restrictions. All invited roles may explicitly choose view=approved without privileged owner MFA because it grants only the ordinary member summary. Caller membership/session/deployment, shared configured reader, original deadline, immutable active head and all relevant source/authority/configuration rows are fenced at final delivery. Use the existing activation read quota and audit stream; exact-ID lookup never falls back to a candidate, historical event or another active ID. An unconfigured trusted reader remains closed. The workbench provides a read-only approved-policy entry to invited members; changing selection, failed read, hidden/offline state or expiry withdraws its current indication. It offers no policy write or enablement control.


Configured-delivery implementation follow-through: explicit paired host/programmatic configuration, genuine shared writer/activation/ActivePolicies factory wiring, bounded discovery/options/request/detail APIs, static approved-current versus owner context, exact bootstrap/promote/rollback routes, generic job dispatch and the shared maintenance qualification handler now exist. The 181-case local delivery cohort and seven qualification-service cases pass on the 534-input subject; broader unchanged-source verification is running. The genuine factory test uses owned local DynamoDB, intercepted provider metadata/identity and synthetic trusted login setup; it runs actual installed software and CV checks, never independent physical or AWS qualification. An omitted synthetic bootstrap pin correctly failed until the test supplied explicit target-bound configuration. New qualification workbench screens, candidate/rollback delivered persona journeys, all original approved-by-ID behavior and complete T/W/A acceptance remain unfinished. The current-policy summary supplements the original ordinary-member approved-policy read requirement; it does not waive that requirement. Source-bound report/data/config changes still hold use, activation still disables inspections, and no external launch follows. See [current verification](../evidence/local/configured-policy-verification.json).


Workbench continuation gap identified before UI edits: MemberWorkbench conditionally unmounts panels when changing sections, while useTasks clears its pending-operation map on unmount. Existing panel-local retry intents therefore cannot be assumed to survive navigation. This is a source-observed lifecycle risk; its rendered reproduction is still pending. Delivering qualification/activation screens must preserve exact unconfirmed intent across permitted section changes or require an explicit informed finish/handoff before discarding it. Ordinary readback never settles an uncertain write. Do not silently generate a fresh key/body, persist credentials/raw images in browser storage, trap the user away from stopping/sign-out, or extend the original preview/report lifetime. Inactive/hidden/offline views invalidate current previews/results while keeping the separately scoped immutable retry record; account/session change ends its action authority. Test section change, return to inspections, sign-out/session loss, failed create/transition/step responses, exact retry and explicit finish under A05-Q1–Q4/A07/T58 before claiming the complete rendered role journey.


Final configured-backend local checkpoint: 590 selected cases/1770 phases across 21 modules and twelve independently reconciled owned database cohorts pass on 534 unchanged inputs. The original raw configuration-race oracle also passes unchanged in a separate serialized clean run. Package/source bytes match 100 Python modules and three frontend files; outside-sandbox offline unconfigured ASGI smoke is separate from the genuine configured source/HTTP/shared-worker test. This strengthens local backend evidence only. It does not install the missing workbench journeys, fulfill the ordinary-member approved-by-ID requirement, prove installed configured-worker execution, qualify a physical/independent/target use case or authorize beta. The source-observed unconfirmed-navigation seam remains planned for rendered reproduction before UI edits.


Workbench navigation repair contract (specified before implementation): the rendered policy lost-response scenario reproduces silent section unmount and loss of exact local retry state. A shared in-memory unresolved-work registry will aggregate explicit panel retry state and implicit transport-failed write intents; it stores no body, token, image or credentials. Before section change, return to inspections, sign-out or step-up navigation would discard that state, present an accessible Leave unconfirmed work? decision. Keep working here performs no navigation/write and preserves the original body/key/version. Leave and investigate requires a separate explicit acknowledgment that only local details are discarded, the server may already have committed, and original records must be investigated before another intent; then perform the selected navigation once. No automatic retry, cancellation, renewed authority or changed result follows. Existing panel-specific successful retry/explicit finish removes its unresolved signal; a source/current read alone cannot settle the write. Session/authority loss ends action authority and explains outstanding uncertainty. This repair does not certify all legacy forms, persist retry data across logout/reload, or complete the pending qualification/activation screens. Preserve original browser limits, assertion and trace; test transport and malformed-response uncertainty, canceled departure with identical retry, acknowledged departure, sign-out/return and no added writes.


Historical pre-repair finding, before screen implementation: evaluations._inputs and policy_qualification._inputs require the selected revision to equal the current candidate head latest_revision, while the activation core requires rollback to the exact predecessor policy ID/revision/digest. Consequently a fresh rollback qualification for an older predecessor revision of the same candidate ID is blocked after a newer revision becomes latest. This is a source-observed workflow/contract gap, not a successful rollback test or an exploit. Complete W16/W18 by defining a server-verified exact rollback-target selection propagated through fresh evaluation/preparation/qualification and consumed activation. Preserve ordinary proposal/latest-head fences, exact predecessor matching, current rights/source/role/control/data checks and original budgets. Do not use a generic allow-stale/consumed client override, reuse an expired qualification, or copy the definition into a new draft and call it the original predecessor. Reproduce the same-ID prior-revision case and verify its complete recovery path before claiming rollback or the activation workbench complete; unsupported cases must remain held until then.

### Exact rollback admission contract

W16/W18 repair design, before implementation: an optional closed `rollback_target` in evaluation and qualification requests names `active_version`, `activation_id` and `predecessor_activation_id`. This is an explicit intent, never a client assertion of freshness. Server admission loads the current active head and both immutable events, verifies their hashes and full head/event agreement, requires the named head version/activation and its actual predecessor, and matches the selected candidate ID/revision/digest to that predecessor. The current candidate state version, binding, source, permissions, runtime, DATA and original budgets remain mandatory. Without this intent the ordinary latest-revision fence remains unchanged.

Preparation inherits the intent from its exact original image evaluation; candidate qualification must match that intent and the exact prepared revision/hash, not merely the candidate ID. Qualification emits and seals the intent in its report. Activation accepts such evidence only for rollback to that exact predecessor under the named active version/activation; promote/bootstrap reject rollback-only evidence. Non-rollback reports cannot supply rollback authority. Concurrent head changes invalidate unconsumed work at final fences.

After consumption, the trusted internal context may verify the original anchor through the new immutable rollback event's previous-active version and predecessor event, plus the original target event. It must match the exact consumed qualification and operation. This preserves current live revalidation without accepting a client `consumed` or `allow_stale` flag or renewing evidence. Installed-default rollback still executes fresh software and golden-image checks; it cannot qualify physical use. Candidate rollback additionally requires the complete current evaluation/preparation chain. Implemented and verified scope belongs to BUILD-STATUS; this design alone closes no gate.

Ordinary request compatibility: when rollback intent is absent or explicitly null, canonical evaluation/qualification serialization omits the new field, preserving the preceding ordinary request body and idempotency digest. An explicit target is serialized and changes the intent digest. This is required for exact-intent recovery; adding an optional purpose must not silently change every existing ordinary request.


Exact rollback implementation follow-through: the explicit target model, current-head/predecessor verifier, evaluation and preparation propagation, exact prepared candidate/purpose check, purpose-sealed qualification, activation restriction and internal consumed revalidation now exist locally. Ordinary absent/null purpose preserves the preceding canonical request and hash. The source-bound 81-case local cohort exercises genuine default and full original-image/preparation/candidate rollback, purpose/forgery/staleness denials and revoked original-reviewer live invalidation; original schema/hash failures and the corrected beta test fixture remain retained. See BUILD-STATUS and the rollback receipt for scope and package evidence. Qualification/activation screens and current metadata-to-decision presentation, full current-product verification and provider/physical/human acceptance remain unfinished. A05-RB1 is the canonical rollback child; the historical replay child A05-R1 is unchanged.

### Qualification and activation workbench delivery contract

Missing metadata reported before implementation: qualification detail does not identify its exact candidate/mode/purpose or expose lease/action availability; historical activation receipts omit candidate digest and qualification binding. Extend permitted own/owner aggregate qualification metadata with immutable request candidate ID/revision/state/digest, mode, preparation ID/state/report and nullable exact rollback target. Add remaining lease and original-reviewer advance versus authorized cancellation/retirement availability. Keep source entries and original-request reads original-reviewer-only. Catalog currency remains unchecked until detail is opened. Historical activation receipts identify the immutable candidate digest, qualification ID/report and previous/new versions, without disclosing owner session or source content and without claiming current authority.

New activation events record the exact candidate state version at transition time. A legacy historical event lacking this field returns null; never substitute today's candidate head. When present, this version must be a positive safe integer under existing provenance verification. A nullable older value cannot confirm a new frozen decision that requires an exact version. Read availability is explicit metadata: only the original reviewer receives original-request availability, and detail availability additionally requires a complete current sealed report. Numerical source counts and registry identity do not grant image or entry access.

P03 uses Policy qualification to discover/read own jobs, inspect installed bounds, select an installed-default candidate or derive exact candidate/purpose from a complete permitted preparation and original image-evaluation request, and separately acknowledge synthetic-only scope before creating work. For installed-default rollback, P01 supplies the exact active/predecessor handoff from the owner UI; P03 enters those fields and the old candidate revision, checks current active version, and the server verifies the full target. P03 can read every bounded phase/software page, explicitly advance, cancel or retire, and recover the original request without automatic retry or renewed validity. P01 has aggregate discovery/read/end controls without inherited reviewer privileges.

P01 uses Policy activation to freshly read recent-MFA owner active/control context, the exact candidate revision, and a current qualified report bound to that same candidate/head state and purpose. Read the predecessor receipt/candidate when rollback is selected; compare the exact predecessor digest and current-head handoff with the qualification target. A separately acknowledged preview binds operation, candidate/state/hash, qualification/report, active/control versions and predecessor. Editing inputs, expiry, offline/hidden state or failed reads clears the preview. Each write uses a frozen body/key/version; only a matching historical transition receipt settles that intent. Current reads never automatically settle an uncertain write. Exact retry and an acknowledged finish/leave remain available; no silent resubmission, authority renewal or enablement follows.

Both panels use existing WorkbenchBusy/WorkbenchUnconfirmed and review-deadline mechanisms. No private console, raw JSON payload entry, persisted capabilities, new role, automatic activation or automatic inspection enablement substitutes for the documented journeys. Historical/current/held, synthetic origin, failure/unknown denominators and missing physical/independent/human evidence must remain visible. Provide native labels, keyboard/focus, zoom, accessible status and bounded case pagination. Validate role-denial, malformed/stale metadata, both qualification modes, all three transitions, exact retry after transport/malformed replies, source/config/version changes, expiry/interruption, cancellation/retirement and guarded navigation before claiming rendered delivery.

Additional upstream UI gap reported before implementation: the existing comparison form is latest-only and cannot attach the already supported exact rollback purpose, preventing a rendered non-default candidate rollback. Extend its explicit reviewer selectors with optional owner-handoff target and exact predecessor revision; ordinary comparisons remain latest-only. Freshly read the selected immutable candidate, complete permitted dataset, optional candidate recipe and current active version, then freeze/display the target with the original diagnostic/measurement acknowledgments. Creation confirmation and original-request recovery retain the exact target. Evaluation aggregate metadata includes recorded candidate state/purpose and original-request availability without adding source privileges. A complete current original-image comparison can seed preparation; completed preparation can seed candidate qualification; an owner may seed an activation review from a qualified report. Each handoff uses the existing informed-leave guard, submits no work and carries no acknowledgment or renewed authority across stages.


## Admission factory, liveness and local resource ownership — implementation contract

Gap reported October 16 before implementation: explicit HTTP/worker adapters exist but the configured factory/host does not activate them. Shutdown currently waits only for maintenance, so it does not establish that foreground/background HTTP users have stopped before shared clients close. Ordinary startup must not create absent admission state, because absence cannot distinguish a new installation from incomplete restoration.

Required factory behavior: typed explicit admission configuration supplies max_active, max_lease_ms, http_lease_ms, worker_lease_ms, attempts and enrollment_sha256, with no operational defaults. Durations stay within the coordinator's existing 300000ms bound. Beta configuration without these controls is rejected before SDK use. Existing test/synthetic configuration without the optional section retains its historical non-release scope. One exact coordinator, worker runner and local resource owner are shared by the actual factory. Startup performs a read-only check of original enrollment after the disabled runtime starts; missing, corrupt or mismatched state fails startup, and an existing HELD gate is never reopened. Explicit first enrollment remains a separate admitted provisioning operation, not automatic startup. An enrollment hash is an external reference, not authenticated approval.

New ordinary work is held while durable active entries belong to another runtime incarnation. Such entries are not discarded on expiry or restart. Original executors may release exact finished bookkeeping; no force-clear, dead-host reconciliation or resume authority is introduced.

Only exact GET/HEAD /health/live may return a static non-data response outside durable admission. It must not invoke downstream routes, metadata, authentication or SDK clients; query/header content cannot select a bypass. Other HTTP and ordinary maintenance poll work use the same coordinator. Readiness remains denied. OAuth query scrubbing remains the outermost user middleware.

A separate process-local resource owner tracks each HTTP/worker lifetime before its first admission read through awaited application/background work and final release attempt. It grants no privacy drain proof. Shutdown stops local admission, then waits for maintenance and local scopes within one existing maintenance_drain_ms budget. Shared clients close exactly once only after both actually exit; otherwise startup/shutdown reports failure and cleanup is deferred. Cancellation, termination or a returned deferred worker result leaves uncertain ownership held, never inferred stopped from time or an empty durable counter. No automatic restart of a used application incarnation.

Acceptance requires read-only existing/held enrollment and absent-state refusal; stale-incarnation denial; typed host bounds before SDK; actual factory shared wiring; non-data liveness with held/missing metadata; HTTP/background/worker shutdown races and exact deferred cleanup; OAuth redaction order; preserved legacy test/synthetic behavior. Pure/model checks cannot establish actual-store or installed-host acceptance; these remain required after the running canonical full regression finishes. No provider, deletion-control, enrollment-provisioning, dead-host, physical or human completion is implied.


Factory shutdown cancellation clarification, reported before repair: local stop, maintenance stop signaling and race-safe deferred cleanup registration must occur synchronously before the first awaited drain. Structured cancellation must not skip bounded graceful handling; direct task cancellation may end the wait but must mark the incarnation failed and retain cleanup callbacks until actual scopes exit. No timeout, cancellation or forcible host termination proves privacy drain or successful host recovery. Maintenance stopped before its thread starts must be terminal for resource ownership without joining an unstarted thread. Existing timeout/resource ownership constraints remain unchanged.


Actual-store regression clarification, reported before repair: shared clients must not close from a maintenance thread's final callback while that thread is still alive. Register actual thread-exit observation before awaiting shutdown, and combine it with local-scope exit and an explicit deferred-cleanup request; a normal successful drain closes synchronously after join. An observed timeout remains a failed shutdown even if the worker exits before later processing-stop notification is registered. A single owned non-SDK observer may wait for physical thread exit; it grants no privacy proof and cannot close resources while either owner remains active. Beta target-validation test arrangements must explicitly supply the now-required typed admission section; preserve every existing target/schema rejection assertion.


## Held inventory read-set integration — contract reported before implementation

Gap: family discovery adapters require a real admitted inventory/read set, while the current standalone collector/reconciler accepts a caller checkpoint and does not itself establish durable drain. Bind that prerequisite through a private read-only service using the exact PersistentAdmission, repository/client/runtime and shared ResourceOwner. It creates no hold, enrollment, owner permission, public endpoint, cleanup job, metadata write, object fetch or erasure authority.

Constructor inputs must explicitly supply typed InventoryLimits, positive exact-integer snapshot/payload budgets, a shared local resource owner and trusted monotonic tick; no operational defaults. A read supplies the original canonical request UUID, exact scope SHA-256 and original absolute monotonic deadline. Acquire local resource ownership before the first admission read. Require existing HELD state, disabled current runtime and an actually empty durable ordinary-work registry. Expired/crashed entries remain active; wrong request/scope, absent/corrupt controls and changed target hold the read.

Capture the exact immutable drain proof, then collect and privately reconcile both tables with complete follow-up enumeration. Every supplied checkpoint must revalidate that same proof, not obtain a replacement. Use one checked nondecreasing monotonic clock across collection, rehydration, follow-up and final binding; retain all existing independent page/record/summary/snapshot/payload limits. Recheck the exact proof after preparing a deterministic payload-free digest binding request/scope, target, control references and inventory summary. Any change, deadline, cancellation, unsupported response or exhausted budget yields no accepted partial artifact. Ordinary configured work cannot enter while this hold exists; privileged/unregistered writers and complete independent-entrypoint assurance remain separate obligations.

Return an immutable private artifact containing the original proof, payload-free inventory and detached raw read set hidden from repr, plus its binding digest. It is input to future schema-aware classification, never proof of ownership, a globally atomic snapshot, authenticated owner approval, legal erasure or completed requester fulfillment. Mutating/forging such a Python value grants no action; later preview/confirmation/commit must separately revalidate source versions and permissions.

Local ownership remains held through all synchronous work and final checks. A known completed exception may release the local scope; cancellation/termination retains uncertain ownership and marks the service pending, denying another read until explicit reconciliation. Shutdown may stop new local scopes but cannot close shared clients mid-read. Native and installed proof plus actual control-plane/factory wiring are required before accepting this component in a complete cleanup workflow; canonical source stays frozen during the running full regression.


Held-inventory critical-control clarification, reported before repair: the enumerated inventory must contain live entries for the exact gate, active registry and runtime references in the original drain proof, with matching revisions and canonical payload digests. Rechecking those controls through a separate read cannot certify an enumeration that omitted them. Reject a missing, erased or mismatched control entry before rehydrating private family inputs; preserve the original inventory/proof and return no partial result. This strengthens the read-set prerequisite, without creating semantic closure or deletion authority.


Interrupted full-regression recovery, October 17: a resource-admission hold before database/test creation is not a failed test or a live controller. Preserve the terminal original attempt and stale raw status. Reuse a completed prefix only after exact original source/runtime/profile, collection/order, every phase/JUnit/origin/SDK and cleanup are independently reconciled. Continue only unexecuted original whole-module cohorts, with fresh unchanged memory admission, exclusive execution claims and each original timeout; do not rerun completed work, renew a full collection or enlarge the aggregate process budget. All source/runtime inputs stay frozen. Full acceptance requires a separate exact composite audit of the complete original inventory; prefix/model checks cannot substitute. The current 720-input recovery retains nine original passing cohorts and resumes 29, totaling 38 original 828-second allowances / 31,464 seconds within 31,500. A copied auditor's stale range/case/phase constants remain diagnostic evidence; tested current-count validation does not alter product or release authority.

Internal verification scheduling clarification, reported before harness changes: a failed parent full run does not authorize release or count as acceptance. After its complete cleanup audit, an isolated local component may nevertheless be tested on the exact pinned parent source when the original failing case and complete original cohort have both been replayed unchanged, all cleanup is verified, and the unresolved baseline failure remains explicit. This permits diagnostics and component evidence only. No case, deadline, assertion, full-product check, deployment condition or beta gate is waived; a fresh whole-current check remains required after integration.


Local integration boundary on a recorded failed baseline: the three held-readset files add a private service and tests without changing any of the 626 existing source inputs. After exact source/installed/native/original verification and parent review, they may be transferred locally with all previous source/evidence backed up and the failed parent full gate retained. This is reversible code integration, not workflow/release acceptance. Canonical selected verification and a fresh whole-current run remain mandatory; no later pass may be attributed to fixing the earlier unrelated vision timeout without causal evidence.


## Server-derived privacy classification and closure — contract before implementation

Gap reported October 16 before implementation, PP09/W20/W21/W22: the private held-inventory service supplies a complete, version-bound read set, but each existing lineage parser still requires caller-selected family inputs. There is no server-owned classifier that derives those inputs and accounts for every inventoried record. A complete read set and a valid parser result cannot establish that omitted copies, shared artifacts or historical replay outputs were handled. This contract specifies the missing integration; it does not announce implemented cleanup or owner authority.

### Admission and exact scope

The eventual requester workflow first authenticates an enabled owner with recent MFA and admits an exact inspection scope under the existing role/session/version rules. Its request, preview, confirmation, worker and receipt must preserve that same scope; none accepts arbitrary database keys, row payloads, family lists, graph edges or a caller-supplied “unrelated” classification. Inspection identifiers are selectors, never proof of ownership. Subject-wide requests need their own complete, versioned subject-to-inspection resolution and separate preview; they cannot silently reuse an inspection-only promise.

The classifier consumes the server-produced held read set for the admitted request, deployment, tables and inventory digest. Before accepting a result, its orchestration revalidates the original drain proof, target, source versions, scope and original absolute deadline. The detached Python artifact and its digest are not capabilities. Revalidate public/mutable configuration and raw row contents; never trust a caller-created HeldInventory instance. Acquire and retain the shared local resource owner before the first control/store read through classification and final checks. Cancellation or uncertain executor completion retains pending ownership and prevents a new request from treating the work as finished. Canonical source remains frozen during the active whole-current regression.

### Complete corpus accounting

Build one bounded private index from every live inventory row and every retained marker. Bind each identity to its original logical table/key, revision and canonical payload digest or validated marker references. Reject duplicates, missing/extra identities, payload changes, malformed versions, mismatched target/proof and alias mutation. Index construction and subsequent joins use explicit independent record, payload, edge/work and deadline limits; no unbounded recursive traversal, repeated full Cartesian joins or default unlimited budget. Budget exhaustion yields a held result with no accepted partial plan. Raw payloads, object keys, signed authorities, identities and source values stay private and absent from repr, ordinary logs and public summaries.

Each original inventory entry receives exactly one accounting disposition, with deterministic reason and policy version:

- **AFFECTED**: a supported schema-specific content/copy relationship places this record in the requested inspection's dependency closure. This is a cleanup candidate, not permission to erase it.
- **SHARED**: a supported record contains relevant copied content and also content/obligations outside the requested scope, or belongs to a dependent artifact with other subjects. Record the typed relationships and collateral consequences; never silently relabel it unrelated or wholly owned.
- **CONTEXT**: a validated reference required to interpret or fence the affected records, such as runtime, station, immutable recipe, calibration or policy. A transaction check alone cannot promote context to ownership or cause traversal through every record sharing that context.
- **UNRELATED**: an explicit supported family policy validates why the complete record contains no in-scope content/copy relationship. A prefix, missing inspection_id, unfamiliar shape or failed parser cannot establish this disposition.
